




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

dīghanikāyai


sīlakkhandhavaggaṭīkā


ganthārambhakathāvaṇṇanā

saṃvaṇṇanārambhai  ratanattayavandanā saṃvaṇṇaitabbassa dhammassa pabhavanissayavisuddhipaṭivaidanatthaṃ, taṃ pana dhammasaṃvaṇṇanāsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ bahumānuppādanatthaṃ, taṃ sammadaiva taisaṃ uggahadhāraṇādik‍kamaladdhabbāya sammāpaṭipattiyā sabbahitasukhanipphādanatthaṃ| atha vā maṅgalabhāvatau, sabbakiriyāsu pubbakic‍cabhāvatau, paṇḍitaihi sammācaritabhāvatau, āyatiṃ paraisaṃ diṭṭhānugatiāpaj‍janatau ca saṃvaṇṇanāyaṃ ratanattayapaṇāmakiriyā| atha vā ratanattayapaṇāmakaraṇaṃ pūjanīyapūjāpuñ‍ñavisaisanibbattanatthaṃ, taṃ attanau yathāladdhasampattinimittakassa kammassa balānuppādanatthaṃ, antarā ca tassa asaṅkaucanatthaṃ, tadubhayaṃ anantarāyaina aṭṭhakathāya parisamāpanatthaṃ| idamaiva ca payaujanaṃ ācariyaina idhādhippaitaṃ| tathā hi vakkhati – ‘‘iti mai pasan‍namatinau…pai॰… tassānubhāvainā’’ti| vatthuttayapūjā hi niratisayapuñ‍ñakkhaittasambuddhiyā aparimaiyyappabhāvau  puñ‍ñātisayauti bahuvidhantarāyaipi laukasan‍nivāsai antarāyanibandhanasakalasaṃkilaisaviddhaṃsanāya pahauti, bhayādiupaddavañ‍ca nivāraiti| yathāha –

‘‘pūjārahai pūjayatau, buddhai yadi va sāvakai’’tiādi (dha॰ pa॰ 1.195; apa॰ 1.10.1), tathā –

‘‘yai  bhikkhavai buddhai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā| aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hautī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 4.34; itivu॰ 90)|

‘‘buddhauti  kittayantassa, kāyai bhavati yā pīti|

varamaiva hi sā pīti, kasiṇainapi jambudīpassa||

dhammauti…pai॰… saṅghauti…pai॰… dīpassā’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.6)|

tathā –

‘‘yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausa…pai॰… na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hautī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 6.10; 11.11),

‘‘arañ‍ñai rukkhamūlai vā…pai॰…

bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā,

laumahaṃsau na haissatī’’ti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.249) ca

tattha yassa vatthuttayassa vandanaṃ kattukāmau, tassa guṇātisayayaugasandassanatthaṃ ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’ntiādinā gāthattayamāha| guṇātisayayaugaina hi vandanārahabhāvau, vandanārahai ca katā vandanā yathādhippaitappayaujanaṃ sādhaitīti| tattha yassā daisanāya saṃvaṇṇanaṃ kattukāmau, sā na vinayadaisanā viya karuṇāppadhānā, nāpi abhidhammadaisanā viya pañ‍ñāppadhānā, atha khau karuṇāpañ‍ñāppadhānāti tadubhayappadhānamaiva tāva sammāsambuddhassa thaumanaṃ kātuṃ taṃmūlakattā saisaratanānaṃ ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

tattha  kiratīti karuṇā, paradukkhaṃ vikkhipati, apanaitīti atthau| atha vā kiṇātīti karuṇā, paradukkhai sati kāruṇikaṃ hiṃsati, vibādhatīti atthau, paradukkhai sati sādhūnaṃ  kampanaṃ hadayakhaidaṃ karautīti vā karuṇā| atha vā kamiti sukhaṃ, taṃ rundhatīti karuṇā| aisā hi paradukkhāpanayanakāmatālakkhaṇā, attasukhanirapaikkhatāya kāruṇikānaṃ sukhaṃ rundhati vibandhatīti| karuṇāya sītalaṃ karuṇāsītalaṃ, karuṇāsītalaṃ hadayaṃ assāti karuṇāsītalahadayau, taṃ karuṇāsītalahadayaṃ| tattha kiñ‍cāpi paraisaṃ hitaupasaṃhārasukhādiaparihānicchanasabhāvatāya, byāpādāratīnaṃ ujuvipac‍canīkatāya ca sattasantānagatasantāpavicchaidanākārappavattiyā maittāmuditānampi cittasītalabhāvakāraṇatā upalabbhati, tathāpi  dukkhāpanayanākārappavattiyā parūpatāpāsahanarasā avihiṃsābhūtā karuṇā visaisaina bhagavatau cittassa cittapassaddhi viya sītībhāvanimittanti vuttaṃ ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’nti| karuṇāmukhaina vā maittāmuditānampi hadayasītalabhāvakāraṇatā vuttāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

atha vā asādhāraṇañāṇavisaisanibandhanabhūtā sātisayaṃ niravasaisañ‍ca sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ viya savisayabyāpitāya mahākaruṇābhāvaṃ upagatā karuṇāva bhagavatau atisayaina hadayasītalabhāvahaitūti āha ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’nti| atha vā satipi maittāmuditānaṃ sātisayai hadayasītībhāvanibandhanattai sakalabuddhaguṇavisaisakāraṇatāya tāsampi kāraṇanti karuṇāva bhagavatau hadayasītalabhāvakāraṇaṃ vuttā| karuṇānidānā hi sabbaipi buddhaguṇā| karuṇānubhāvanibbāpiyamānasaṃsāradukkhasantāpassa hi bhagavatau paradukkhāpanayanakāmatāya anaikānipi asaṅkhaiyyāni kappānaṃ akilantarūpassaiva niravasaisabuddhakaradhammasambharaṇaniyatassa samadhigatadhammādhipataiyyassa ca san‍nihitaisupi sattasaṅkhārasamupanītahadayūpatāpanimittaisu na īsakampi  cittasītībhāvassañ‍ñathattamahausīti| aitasmiñ‍ca atthavikappai tīsupi avatthāsu bhagavatau karuṇā saṅgahitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

pajānātīti pañ‍ñā, yathāsabhāvaṃ pakāraihi paṭivijjhatīti atthau| pañ‍ñāva ñaiyyāvaraṇappahānatau pakāraihi dhammasabhāvāvajautanaṭṭhaina paj‍jautauti pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau, savāsanappahānatau visaisaina hataṃ samugghāṭitaṃ vihataṃ, pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautaina vihataṃ pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihataṃ| muyhanti taina, sayaṃ vā muyhati, mauhanamattamaiva vā tanti mauhau, avij‍jā, svaiva visayasabhāvapaṭicchādanatau andhakārasarikkhatāya tamau viyāti tamau, pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatau mauhatamau aitassāti pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatamau, taṃ pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatamaṃ| sabbaisampi hi khīṇāsavānaṃ satipi pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautaina avij‍jāndhakārassa vihatabhāvai saddhādhimuttaihi viya diṭṭhippattānaṃ sāvakaihi, pac‍caikasambuddhaihi ca savāsanappahānaina sammāsambuddhānaṃ kilaisappahānassa visaisau vij‍jatīti sātisayaina avij‍jāppahānaina bhagavantaṃ thaumaintau āha ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti|

atha  vā antaraina paraupadaisaṃ attanau santānai ac‍cantaṃ avij‍jāndhakāravigamassa nibbattitattā, tattha ca sabbañ‍ñutāya, balaisu ca vasībhāvassa samadhigatattā, parasantatiyañ‍ca dhammadaisanātisayānubhāvaina sammadaiva tassa pavattitattā bhagavāva visaisatau mauhatamavigamaina thaumaitabbauti āha ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti| imasmiñ‍ca atthavikappai ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau’’ti padaina bhagavatau paṭivaidhapañ‍ñā viya daisanāpañ‍ñāpi sāmañ‍ñaniddaisaina aikasaisanayaina vā saṅgahitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

atha  vā bhagavatau ñāṇassa ñaiyyapariyantikattā sakalañaiyyadhammasabhāvābaudhanasamatthaina anāvaraṇañāṇasaṅkhātaina pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautaina sabbañaiyyadhammasabhāvacchādakassa mauhandhakārassa vidhamitattā anañ‍ñasādhāraṇau bhagavatau mauhatamavināsauti katvā vuttaṃ ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti| aittha ca mauhatamavidhamanantai adhigatattā anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ kāraṇūpacāraina sakasantānai mauhatamavidhamanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| abhinīhārasampattiyā savāsanappahānamaiva hi kilaisānaṃ ‘‘ñaiyyāvaraṇappahāna’’nti, parasantānai pana mauhatamavidhamanassa kāraṇabhāvatau anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ ‘‘mauhatamavidhamana’’nti vuc‍catīti|

kiṃ pana kāraṇaṃ avij‍jāvigghātau yaivaikau pahānasampattivasaina bhagavatau thaumanānimittaṃ gayhati, na pana sātisayaniravasaisakilaisappahānanti? tappahānavacanainaiva tadaikaṭṭhatāya sakalasaṃkilaisagaṇasamugghātajautitabhāvatau| na hi sau tādisau kilaisau atthi, yau niravasaisaavij‍jāppahānaina na pahīyatīti| atha vā vij‍jā viya sakalakusaladhammasamuppattiyā niravasaisākusaladhammanibbattiyā, saṃsārappavattiyā ca avij‍jā padhānakāraṇanti tabbigghātavacanaina sakalasaṃkilaisagaṇasamugghātau vuttauyaiva hautīti vuttaṃ ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti|

narā ca amarā ca narāmarā, saha narāmaraihīti sanarāmarau, sanarāmarau ca sau laukau cāti sanarāmaralaukau, tassa garuti sanarāmaralaukagaru, taṃ sanarāmaralaukagaruṃ| aitaina daivamanussānaṃ  viya tadavasiṭṭhasattānampi yathārahaṃ guṇavisaisāvahatau bhagavatau upakāritaṃ  dassaiti| na caittha padhānāpadhānabhāvau caudaitabbau| añ‍ñau hi saddak‍kamau, añ‍ñau atthak‍kamau| aidisaisu hi samāsapadaisu  padhānampi appadhānaṃ viya niddisīyati yathā – ‘‘sarājikāya parisāyā’’ti (apa॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.82)| kāmañ‍caittha sattasaṅkhārabhājanavasaina tividhau laukau, garubhāvassa pana adhippaitattā garukaraṇasamatthassaiva yuj‍janatau sattalaukassavasaina atthau gahaitabbau| sau hi laukiyanti aittha puñ‍ñapāpāni tabbipākau cāti ‘‘laukau’’ti vuc‍cati| amaraggahaṇaina caittha upapattidaivā adhippaitā|

atha vā samūhatthau lauka-saddau samudāyavasaina laukīyati pañ‍ñāpīyatīti| saha naraihīti sanarā, sanarā ca tai amarā caiti sanarāmarā, taisaṃ laukauti sanarāmaralaukauti purimanayainaiva yaujaitabbaṃ| amara-saddaina caittha visuddhidaivāpi saṅgayhanti| tai hi maraṇābhāvatau paramatthatau amarā| narāmarānaṃyaiva ca gahaṇaṃ uk‍kaṭṭhaniddaisavasaina, yathā – ‘‘satthā daivamanussāna’’nti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.157)| tathā hi sabbānatthapariharaṇapubbaṅgamāya niravasaisahitasukhavidhānatapparāya niratisayāya payaugasampattiyā sadaivamanussāya pajāya ac‍cantupakāritāya, aparimitanirupamappabhāvaguṇavisaisasamaṅgitāya ca sabbasattuttamau bhagavā aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ uttamaṃ gāravaṭṭhānaṃ, taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘sanarāmaralaukagaru’’nti|

saubhanaṃ gataṃ gamanaṃ aitassāti sugatau| bhagavatau hi vainaiyyajanupasaṅkamanaṃ aikantaina taisaṃ hitasukhanipphādanatau saubhanaṃ, tathā lakkhaṇānubyañ‍jana (dī॰ ni॰ 2.33; 3.198-200; ma॰ ni॰ 2.385, 386) paṭimaṇḍitarūpakāyatāyadutavilambita- khalitānukaḍḍhananippīḷanuk‍kuṭikakuṭilākulatādidausarahitaṃ vilāsitarājahaṃsavasabhavāraṇamigarājagamanaṃ kāyagamanaṃ  ñāṇagamanañ‍ca vipulanimmalakaruṇāsativīriyādiguṇavisaisasahitamabhinīhāratau yāva mahābaudhi anavaj‍jatāya saubhanamaivāti|

atha vā sayambhuñāṇaina sakalampi laukaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayavasaina parijānantau ñāṇaina sammā gatau avagatauti sugatau| tathā laukasamudayaṃ  pahānābhisamayavasaina pajahantau anuppattidhammataṃ āpādaintau sammā gatau atītauti sugatau| laukaniraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikiriyābhisamayavasaina sammā gatau adhigatauti sugatau| laukaniraudhagāminipaṭipadaṃ bhāvanābhisamayavasaina sammā gatau paṭipan‍nauti sugatau| sautāpattimaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā, tai kilaisai na punaiti, na pac‍caiti, na pac‍cāgacchatīti sugatautiādinā nayaina ayamatthau vibhāvaitabbau| atha vā sundaraṃ ṭhānaṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ nibbānamaiva vā gatau adhigatauti sugatau| yasmā vā bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasañhitaṃ vinaiyyānaṃ yathārahaṃ kālayuttamaiva ca dhammaṃ bhāsati, tasmā sammā gadatīti sugatau, da-kārassa ta-kāraṃ katvā| iti saubhanagamanatādīhi sugatau, taṃ sugataṃ|

puñ‍ñapāpakammaihi upapaj‍janavasaina gantabbatau gatiyau, upapattibhavavisaisā| tā pana nirayādivasaina pañ‍cavidhā, tāhi sakalassāpi bhavagāmikammassa ariyamaggādhigamaina avipākārahabhāvakaraṇaina nivattitattā bhagavā pañ‍cahipi gatīhi suṭṭhu muttau visaṃyuttauti āha – ‘‘gativimutta’’nti| aitaina bhagavatau katthacipi gatiyā apariyāpan‍nataṃ dassaiti, yatau bhagavā ‘‘daivātidaivau’’ti vuc‍cati, tainaivāha –

‘‘yaina daivūpapatyassa, gandhabbau vā vihaṅgamau|

yakkhattaṃ yaina gacchaiyyaṃ, manussattañ‍ca abbajai|

tai mayhaṃ āsavā khīṇā, viddhastā vinaḷīkatā’’ti|| (a॰ ni॰ 4.36)|

taṃtaṃgatisaṃvattanakānañhi  kammakilaisānaṃ aggamaggaina baudhimūlaiyaiva suppahīnattā natthi bhagavatau gatipariyāpan‍natāti ac‍cantamaiva bhagavā sabbabhavayaunigativiñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitisattāvāsasattanikāyaihi suparimuttau, taṃ gativimuttaṃ| vandaiti namāmi, thaumaimīti vā atthau|

atha vā gativimuttanti anupādisaisanibbānadhātuppattiyā bhagavantaṃ thaumaiti| aittha hi dvīhākāraihi bhagavatau thaumanā vaiditabbā – attahitasampattitau, parahitapaṭipattitau ca| taisu attahitasampatti anāvaraṇañāṇādhigamatau, savāsanānaṃ sabbaisaṃ kilaisānaṃ ac‍cantappahānatau, anupādisaisanibbānappattitau ca vaiditabbā| parahitapaṭipatti lābhasak‍kārādinirapaikkhacittassa  sabbadukkhaniyyānikadhammadaisanātau, viruddhaisupi nic‍caṃ hitajjhāsayatau, ñāṇaparipākakālāgamanatau ca| sā panaittha āsayatau payaugatau ca duvidhā parahitapaṭipatti, tividhā ca attahitasampatti pakāsitā hauti| kathaṃ? ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’nti aitaina āsayatau parahitapaṭipatti, sammā gadanatthaina sugata-saddaina payaugatau parahitapaṭipatti, ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatamaṃ gativimutta’’nti aitaihi catusac‍capaṭivaidhatthaina ca sugata-saddaina tividhāpi  attahitasampatti, avasiṭṭhaina, ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti aitaina ca sabbāpi attahitasampattiparahitapaṭipatti pakāsitā hautīti|

atha vā tīhākāraihi bhagavatau thaumanā vaiditabbā – haitutau, phalatau, upakāratau ca| tattha haitu mahākaruṇā, sā paṭhamapadaina nidassitā| phalaṃ catubbidhaṃ – ñāṇasampadā, pahānasampadā, ānubhāvasampadā, rūpakāyasampadā cāti| tāsu ñāṇappahānasampadā dutiyapadaina sac‍cappaṭivaidhatthaina ca sugata-saddaina pakāsitā haunti| ānubhāvasampadā tatiyapadaina, rūpakāyasampadā  yathāvuttakāyagamanasaubhanatthaina sugata-saddaina, lakkhaṇānubyañ‍janapāripūriyā (dī॰ ni॰ 2.33; 3.198-200; ma॰ ni॰ 2.385-386) vinā tadabhāvatau| upakārau antaraṃ abāhiraṃ karitvā tividhayānamukhaina vimuttidhammadaisanā, sau sammā gadanatthaina sugata-saddaina pakāsitau hautīti vaiditabbaṃ|

tattha ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’nti aitaina sammāsambaudhiyā mūlaṃ dassaiti| mahākaruṇāsañ‍cauditamānasau hi bhagavā saṃsārapaṅkatau sattānaṃ samuddharaṇatthaṃ katābhinīhārau anupubbaina pāramiyau pūraitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ adhigatauti karuṇā sammāsambaudhiyā mūlaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti aitaina sammāsambaudhiṃ dassaiti| anāvaraṇañāṇapadaṭṭhānañhi maggañāṇaṃ, maggañāṇapadaṭṭhānañ‍ca anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ ‘‘sammāsambaudhī’’ti vuc‍catīti| sammā gadanatthaina sugata-saddaina sammāsambaudhiyā paṭipattiṃ dassaiti, līnuddhac‍capatiṭṭhānāyūhanakāmasukhal‍likattakilamathānuyauga- sassatucchaidābhinivaisādiantadvayarahitāya karuṇāpañ‍ñāpariggahitāya  majjhimāya paṭipattiyā pakāsanatau sugata-saddassa| itaraihi sammāsambaudhiyā padhānāppadhānabhaidaṃ payaujanaṃ dassaiti| saṃsāramahaughatau sattasantāraṇañ‍caittha padhānaṃ payaujanaṃ, tadañ‍ñamappadhānaṃ| taisu padhānaina parahitappaṭipattiṃ dassaiti, itaraina attahitasampattiṃ, tadubhayaina attahitāya paṭipan‍nādīsu (pu॰ pa॰ 24, 173) catūsu puggalaisu bhagavatau catutthapuggalabhāvaṃ dassaiti| taina ca anuttaradakkhiṇaiyyabhāvaṃ uttamavandanīyabhāvaṃ, attanau ca vandanakiriyāya khaittaṅgatabhāvaṃ dassaiti|

aittha ca karuṇāggahaṇaina laukiyaisu mahaggatabhāvappattāsādhāraṇaguṇadīpanatau bhagavatau sabbalaukiyaguṇasampatti  dassitā hauti, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇapadaṭṭhānamaggañāṇadīpanatau sabbalaukuttaraguṇasampatti| tadubhayaggahaṇasiddhau hi atthau ‘‘sanarāmaralaukagaru’’ntiādinā vipañ‍cīyatīti| karuṇāggahaṇaina ca upagamanaṃ nirupak‍kilaisaṃ  dassaiti, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina apagamanaṃ| tathā karuṇāggahaṇaina laukasamañ‍ñānurūpaṃ bhagavatau pavattiṃ dassaiti, laukavauhāravisayattā karuṇāya, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina samañ‍ñāyānavidhāvanaṃ| sabhāvānavabaudhaina hi dhammānaṃ samañ‍ñaṃ atidhāvitvā sattādiparāmasanaṃ hautīti| tathā karuṇāggahaṇaina mahākaruṇāsamāpattivihāraṃ dassaiti, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina tīsu kālaisu appaṭihatañāṇaṃ, catusac‍cañāṇaṃ, catuppaṭisambhidāñāṇaṃ, catuvaissāraj‍jañāṇaṃ| karuṇāggahaṇaina mahākaruṇāsamāpattiñāṇassa gahitattā saisāsādhāraṇañāṇāni, cha abhiñ‍ñā, aṭṭhasu parisāsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.151) akampanañāṇāni, dasa balāni, cuddasa buddhañāṇāni, sauḷasa ñāṇacariyā, aṭṭhārasa buddhadhammā, (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.305; vibha॰ mūla॰ ṭī॰ ganthārambhavaṇṇanāya) catucattārīsa ñāṇavatthūni, (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.34) sattasattati ñāṇavatthūnīti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.34) aivamādīnaṃ anaikaisaṃ pañ‍ñāppabhaidānaṃ vasaina ñāṇacāraṃ dassaiti|

tathā karuṇāggahaṇaina caraṇasampattiṃ, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina vij‍jāsampattiṃ| karuṇāggahaṇaina sattādhipatitā, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina dhammādhipatitā| karuṇāggahaṇaina laukanāthabhāvau, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina attanāthabhāvau| tathā karuṇāggahaṇaina pubbakāribhāvau, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina katañ‍ñutā| tathā karuṇāggahaṇaina aparantapatā, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina anattantapatā| karuṇāggahaṇaina vā buddhakaradhammasiddhi, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina buddhabhāvasiddhi| tathā karuṇāggahaṇaina paraisaṃ tāraṇaṃ, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina sayaṃ tāraṇaṃ| tathā karuṇāggahaṇaina sabbasattaisu anuggahacittatā, pañ‍ñāggahaṇaina sabbadhammaisu virattacittatā dassitā hauti| sabbaisañ‍ca  buddhaguṇānaṃ karuṇā ādi, tan‍nidānabhāvatau| pañ‍ñā pariyausānaṃ, tatau uttarikaraṇīyābhāvatau| iti ādipariyausānadassanaina sabbai buddhaguṇā dassitā haunti| tathā karuṇāggahaṇaina sīlakkhandhapubbaṅgamau samādhikkhandhau dassitau hauti| karuṇānidānañhi sīlaṃ, tatau pāṇātipātādiviratippavattitau, sā ca jhānattayasampayauginīti| pañ‍ñāvacanaina pañ‍ñākkhandhau| sīlañ‍ca sabbabuddhaguṇānamādi, samādhi majjhai, pañ‍ñā pariyausānanti| aivampi ādimajjhapariyausānakalyāṇā sabbai buddhaguṇā dassitā haunti, nayatau dassitattā| aisau aiva hi niravasaisatau buddhaguṇānaṃ dassanupāyau, yadidaṃ nayaggāhaṇaṃ| añ‍ñathā kau nāma samatthau bhagavatau guṇai anupadaṃ niravasaisatau dassaituṃ| tainaivāha –

‘‘buddhaupi buddhassa bhaṇaiyya vaṇṇaṃ,

kappampi cai añ‍ñamabhāsamānau|

khīyaitha  kappau ciradīghamantarai,

vaṇṇau na khīyaitha tathāgatassā’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.304; dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.141; ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.425, udā॰ aṭṭha॰ 53; bu॰ vaṃ॰ aṭṭha॰ 4.4; cariyā॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathāyaṃ, pakiṇṇakakathāyaṃ; apa॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.6.20)|

tainaiva ca āyasmatā sāriputtatthairaināpi buddhaguṇaparicchaidanaṃ pati anuyuttaina ‘‘nau haitaṃ bhantai’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.145) paṭikkhipitvā, ‘‘api ca mai bhantai dhammanvayau viditau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.146) vuttaṃ|

aivaṃ saṅkhaipaina sakalasabbañ‍ñuguṇaihi bhagavantaṃ abhitthavitvā idāni saddhammaṃ thaumaituṃ ‘‘buddhaupī’’tiādimāha| tattha buddhauti kattuniddaisau| buddhabhāvanti kammaniddaisau| bhāvaitvā, sacchikatvāti ca pubbakālakiriyāniddaisau| yanti aniyamatau kammaniddaisau| upagatauti  aparakālakiriyāniddaisau| vandaiti kiriyāniddaisau, tanti  niyamanaṃ| dhammanti vandanakiriyāya kammaniddaisau| gatamalaṃ, anuttaranti ca tabbisaisanaṃ|

tattha buddha-saddassa tāva ‘‘bujjhitā sac‍cānīti buddhau, baudhaitā pajāyāti buddhau’’tiādinā (mahāni॰ 192; cūḷani॰ 95-97; paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.162) niddaisanayaina atthau vaiditabbau| atha vā savāsanāya añ‍ñāṇaniddāya ac‍cantavigamatau, buddhiyā vā vikasitabhāvatau buddhavāti buddhau, jāgaraṇavikasanatthavasaina| atha vā kassacipi ñaiyyadhammassa anavabuddhassa abhāvaina ñaiyyavisaisassa kammabhāvaina aggahaṇatau kammavacanicchāya abhāvaina avagamanatthavasainaiva kattuniddaisau labbhatīti buddhavāti buddhau, yathā ‘‘dikkhitau na dadātī’’ti, atthatau pana pāramitāparibhāvitau sayambhūñāṇaina saha vāsanāya vihataviddhastaniravasaisakilaisau mahākaruṇāsabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādiaparimaiyya guṇagaṇādhārau khandhasantānau buddhau| yathāha –

‘‘buddhauti yau sau bhagavā sayambhū anācariyakau pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu sāmaṃ sac‍cāni abhisambujjhi, tattha ca sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pattau, balaisu ca vasībhāva’’nti (mahāni॰ 192; cūḷani॰ 95-97; paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.162)|

api-saddau sambhāvanai, taina ‘‘aivaṃ guṇavisaisayuttau saupi nāma bhagavā’’ti vakkhamānaguṇai  dhammai sambhāvanaṃ dīpaiti| buddhabhāvanti sammāsambaudhiṃ| bhāvaitvāti uppādaitvā, vaḍḍhaitvā ca| sacchikatvāti pac‍cakkhaṃ katvā| upagatauti pattau, adhigatauti atthau, aitassa ‘‘buddhabhāva’’nti aitaina sambandhau| gatamalanti vigatamalaṃ, niddausanti atthau| vandaiti paṇamāmi, thaumaimi  vā| anuttaranti uttararahitaṃ, laukuttaranti atthau| dhammanti yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānai apāyatau ca, saṃsāratau ca apatamānai katvā dhārayatīti dhammau|

ayañhaittha saṅkhaipatthau – aivaṃ vividhaguṇasaman‍nāgatau buddhaupi bhagavā yaṃ ariyasaṅkhātaṃ dhammaṃ bhāvaitvā, phalanibbānasaṅkhātaṃ pana sacchikatvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ adhigatau, tamaitaṃ buddhānampi buddhabhāvahaitubhūtaṃ sabbadausamalarahitaṃ attanau uttaritarābhāvaina anuttaraṃ paṭivaidhasaddhammaṃ namāmīti| pariyattisaddhammassāpi tappakāsanattā idha saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| atha  vā ‘‘abhidhammanayasamuddaṃ bhāvaitvā adhigacchi, tīṇi piṭakāni sammasī’’ti ca aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttattā pariyattidhammassāpi sacchikiriyāsammasanapariyāyau labbhatīti saupi idha vuttau yaivāti daṭṭhabbau| tathā ‘‘yaṃ dhammaṃ bhāvaitvā, sacchikatvā’’ti ca vuttattā buddhakaradhammabhūtāhi pāramitāhi saha pubbabhāgai adhisīlasikkhādayaupi idha dhamma-saddaina saṅgahitāti vaiditabbā| tāpi hi vigatapaṭipakkhatāya vigatamalā, anañ‍ñasādhāraṇatāya anuttarā cāti| tathā hi sattānaṃ sakalavaṭṭadukkhanissaraṇāya katamahābhinīhārau mahākaruṇādhivāsapaisalajjhāsayau pañ‍ñāvisaisapariyaudātanimmalānaṃ dānadamasañ‍ñamādīnaṃ uttamadhammānaṃ satasahassādhikāni kappānaṃ cattāri asaṅkhaiyyāni sak‍kac‍caṃ nirantaraṃ niravasaisaṃ bhāvanāpac‍cakkhakaraṇaihi kammādīsu adhigatavasībhāvau, acchariyācintaiyyamahānubhāvau, adhisīlaadhicittānaṃ paramuk‍kaṃsapāramippattau bhagavā pac‍cayākārai catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassamukhaina mahāvajirañāṇaṃ paisaitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti|

aittha  ca ‘‘bhāvaitvā’’ti aitaina vij‍jāsampadāya dhammaṃ thaumaiti, ‘sacchikatvā’ti aitaina vimuttisampadāya| tathā paṭhamaina jhānasampadāya, dutiyaina vimaukkhasampadāya| paṭhamaina vā samādhisampadāya, dutiyaina samāpattisampadāya| atha vā paṭhamaina khayañāṇabhāvaina, dutiyaina anuppādañāṇabhāvaina| purimaina vā vij‍jūpamatāya, dutiyaina vajirūpamatāya| purimaina vā virāgasampattiyā, dutiyaina niraudhasampattiyā| tathā paṭhamaina niyyānabhāvaina, dutiyaina nissaraṇabhāvaina| paṭhamaina vā haitubhāvaina, dutiyaina asaṅkhatabhāvaina| paṭhamaina vā dassanabhāvaina, dutiyaina vivaikabhāvaina| paṭhamaina vā adhipatibhāvaina, dutiyaina amatabhāvaina dhammaṃ thaumaiti| atha vā ‘‘yaṃ dhammaṃ bhāvaitvā buddhabhāvaṃ upagatau’’ti aitaina svākkhātatāya dhammaṃ thaumaiti, ‘‘sacchikatvā’’ti  aitaina sandiṭṭhikatāya| tathā purimaina akālikatāya, pacchimaina aihipassikatāya| purimaina vā aupanaiyyikatāya, pacchimaina pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbatāya dhammaṃ thaumaiti|

‘‘gatamala’’nti iminā saṃkilaisābhāvadīpanaina dhammassa parisuddhataṃ dassaiti, ‘‘anuttara’’nti aitaina añ‍ñassa visiṭṭhassa abhāvadīpanaina vipulaparipuṇṇataṃ| paṭhamaina vā pahānasampadaṃ dhammassa dassaiti, dutiyaina pabhāvasampadaṃ| bhāvaitabbatāya vā dhammassa gatamalabhāvau yaujaitabbau| bhāvanāguṇaina hi sau dausānaṃ samugghātakau hautīti| sacchikātabbabhāvaina anuttarabhāvau yaujaitabbau| sacchikiriyānibbattitau hi taduttarikaraṇīyābhāvatau anañ‍ñasādhāraṇatāya anuttarauti| tathā ‘‘bhāvaitvā’’ti aitaina saha pubbabhāgasīlādīhi saikkhā sīlasamādhipañ‍ñākkhandhā dassitā haunti, ‘‘sacchikatvā’’ti aitaina saha asaṅkhatāya dhātuyā asaikkhā sīlasamādhipañ‍ñākkhandhā dassitā hauntīti|

aivaṃ saṅkhaipainaiva sabbadhammaguṇaihi saddhammaṃ abhitthavitvā, idāni ariyasaṅghaṃ thaumaituṃ ‘‘sugatassā’’tiādimāha| tattha sugatassāti sambandhaniddaisau, tassa ‘‘puttāna’’nti aitaina sambandhau| aurasānanti puttavisaisanaṃ| mārasainamathanānanti  aurasaputtabhāvai kāraṇaniddaisau, taina kilaisappahānamaiva bhagavatau aurasaputtabhāvakāraṇaṃ anujānātīti dassaiti| aṭṭhan‍nanti gaṇanaparicchaidaniddaisau, taina ca satipi taisaṃ sattavisaisabhāvaina anaikasatasahassasaṅkhyabhāvai imaṃ gaṇanaparicchaidaṃ nātivattantīti dassaiti, maggaṭṭhaphalaṭṭhabhāvānativattanatau| samūhanti samudāyaniddaisau| ariyasaṅghanti guṇavisiṭṭhasaṅghātabhāvaniddaisau, taina asatipi ariyapuggalānaṃ kāyasāmaggiyaṃ ariyasaṅghabhāvaṃ dassaiti, diṭṭhisīlasāmañ‍ñaina saṃhatabhāvatau| tattha urasi bhavā jātā, saṃvaddhā ca aurasā| yathā hi sattānaṃ aurasaputtā attajātatāya pitusantakassa dāyaj‍jassa visaisaina bhāginau haunti, aivamaitaipi ariyapuggalā sammāsambuddhassa savanantai ariyāya jātiyā jātatāya bhagavatau santakassa vimuttisukhassa, ariyadhammaratanassa ca aikantabhāginauti aurasā viya aurasā| atha vā bhagavatau dhammadaisanānubhāvaina ariyabhūmiṃ auk‍kamamānā, auk‍kantā ca ariyasāvakā bhagavatau urauvāyāmajanitābhijātatāya nippariyāyaina ‘‘aurasaputtā’’ti vattabbataṃ arahanti| sāvakaihi pavattiyamānāpi hi dhammadaisanā bhagavatau ‘‘dhammadaisanā’’ ic‍caiva vuc‍cati, taṃmūlakattā, lakkhaṇādivisaisābhāvatau ca|

yadipi ariyasāvakānaṃ ariyamaggādhigamasamayai bhagavatau viya tadantarāyakaraṇatthaṃ daivaputtamārau, māravāhinī vā na aikantaina apasādaiti, taihi pana apasādaitabbatāya kāraṇai vimathitai taipi vimathitā  aiva nāma hauntīti āha – ‘‘mārasainamathanāna’’nti| imasmiṃ panatthai ‘māramārasainamathanāna’nti vattabbai ‘‘mārasainamathanāna’’nti aikadaisasarūpaikasaisau katauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| atha vā khandhābhisaṅkhāramārānaṃ viya daivaputtamārassāpi guṇamāraṇai  sahāyabhāvūpagamanatau kilaisabalakāyau ‘‘sainā’’ti vuc‍cati| yathāha – ‘‘kāmā tai paṭhamā sainā’’tiādi (su॰ ni॰ 438; mahāni॰ 28, 68; cūḷani॰ 47)| sā ca taihi diyaḍḍhasahassabhaidā, anantabhaidā vā kilaisavāhinī satidhammavicayavīriyasamathādiguṇapaharaṇaihi audhisau vimathitā, vihatā, viddhastā cāti mārasainamathanā, ariyasāvakā| aitaina taisaṃ bhagavatau anujātaputtataṃ dassaiti|

ārakattā kilaisaihi, anayai na iriyanatau, ayai ca iriyanatau ariyā, niruttinayaina| atha vā sadaivakaina laukaina ‘‘saraṇa’’nti araṇīyatau upagantabbatau, upagatānañ‍ca tadatthasiddhitau ariyā, ariyānaṃ saṅghauti ariyasaṅghau, ariyau ca sau, saṅghau cāti vā ariyasaṅghau, taṃ ariyasaṅghaṃ| bhagavatau aparabhāgai buddhadhammaratanānampi samadhigamau saṅgharatanādhīnauti assa ariyasaṅghassa bahūpakārataṃ dassaituṃ idhaiva ‘‘sirasā vandai’’ti vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

aittha ca ‘‘sugatassa aurasānaṃ puttāna’’nti aitaina ariyasaṅghassa pabhavasampadaṃ dassaiti, ‘‘mārasainamathanāna’’nti aitaina pahānasampadaṃ, sakalasaṃkilaisappahānadīpanatau| ‘‘aṭṭhan‍nampi samūha’’nti aitaina ñāṇasampadaṃ, maggaṭṭhaphalaṭṭhabhāvadīpanatau| ‘‘ariyasaṅgha’’nti aitaina pabhavasampadaṃ dassaiti, sabbasaṅghānaṃ aggabhāvadīpanatau| atha vā ‘‘sugatassa aurasānaṃ puttāna’’nti ariyasaṅghassa visuddhanissayabhāvadīpanaṃ, ‘‘mārasainamathanāna’’nti sammāujuñāyasāmīcippaṭipan‍nabhāvadīpanaṃ, ‘‘aṭṭhan‍nampi samūha’’nti āhunaiyyādibhāvadīpanaṃ, ‘‘ariyasaṅgha’’nti anuttarapuñ‍ñakkhaittabhāvadīpanaṃ| tathā ‘‘sugatassa aurasānaṃ puttāna’’nti aitaina ariyasaṅghassa laukuttarasaraṇagamanasabbhāvaṃ dīpaiti| laukuttarasaraṇagamanaina hi tai bhagavatau aurasaputtā jātā| ‘‘mārasainamathanāna’’nti  aitaina abhinīhārasampadāsiddhaṃ pubbabhāgai sammāpaṭipattiṃ dassaiti| katābhinīhārā hi sammā paṭipan‍nā māraṃ, māraparisaṃ vā abhivijinanti| ‘‘aṭṭhan‍nampi samūha’’nti aitaina  viddhastavipakkhai saikkhāsaikkhadhammai dassaiti, puggalādhiṭṭhānaina maggaphaladhammānaṃ pakāsitattā| ‘‘ariyasaṅgha’’nti aggadakkhiṇaiyyabhāvaṃ dassaiti| saraṇagamanañ‍ca sāvakānaṃ sabbaguṇānamādi, sapubbabhāgappaṭipadā saikkhā sīlakkhandhādayau majjhai, asaikkhā sīlakkhandhādayau  pariyausānanti ādimajjhapariyausānakalyāṇā saṅkhaipatau sabbai ariyasaṅghaguṇā pakāsitā haunti|

aivaṃ gāthāttayaina saṅkhaipatau sakalaguṇasaṅkittanamukhaina ratanattayassa paṇāmaṃ katvā, idāni taṃ nipac‍cakāraṃ yathādhippaitai payaujanai pariṇāmaintau ‘‘iti mai’’tiādimāha| tattha ratijananaṭṭhaina ratanaṃ, buddhadhammasaṅghā| taisañhi ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā’’tiādinā yathābhūtaguṇai āvaj‍jantassa amatādhigamahaitubhūtaṃ anappakaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati| yathāha –

‘‘yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti tathāgataṃ ārabbha| ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ, pamuditassa pīti jāyatī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 6.10; a॰ ni॰ 11.11)|

cittīkatādibhāvau vā ratanaṭṭhau| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘cittīkataṃ mahagghañ‍ca, atulaṃ dul‍labhadassanaṃ|

anaumasattaparibhaugaṃ, ratanaṃ taina vuc‍catī’’ti|| (khu॰ pā॰ aṭṭha॰ 6.3; dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.33; su॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.226; mahāni॰ aṭṭha॰ 50)|

cittīkatabhāvādayau  ca anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā buddhādīsu aiva labbhantīti| vandanāva vandanāmayaṃ, yathā ‘‘dānamayaṃ, sīlamaya’’nti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.305; itivu॰ 60; naitti॰ 34)| vandanā caittha kāyavācācittaihi tiṇṇaṃ ratanānaṃ guṇanin‍natā, thaumanā vā| puj‍jabhavaphalanibbattanatau puñ‍ñaṃ, attanau santānaṃ puṇātīti vā| suvihatantarāyauti suṭṭhu vihatantarāyau, aitaina attanau pasādasampattiyā, rattanattayassa  ca khaittabhāvasampattiyā taṃ puñ‍ñaṃ atthappakāsanassa upaghātakaupaddavānaṃ vihananai samatthanti dassaiti| hutvāti pubbakālakiriyā, tassa ‘‘atthaṃ pakāsayissāmī’’ti aitaina sambandhau| tassāti yaṃ ratanattayavandanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, tassa| ānubhāvaināti balaina|

aivaṃ  ratanattayassa nipac‍cakārakaraṇai payaujanaṃ dassaitvā, idāni yassā dhammadaisanāya atthaṃ saṃvaṇṇaitukāmau, tassā tāva guṇābhitthavanavasaina upañ‍ñāpanatthaṃ ‘‘dīghassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha dīghasuttaṅkitassāti dīghappamāṇasuttalakkhitassa, aitaina ‘‘dīghau’’ti ayaṃ imassa āgamassa atthānugatā samañ‍ñāti dassaiti| nanu ca suttāniyaiva āgamau, kassa pana suttaihi aṅkananti? sac‍camaitaṃ paramatthatau, suttāni pana upādāyapañ‍ñattau āgamau| yathā hi atthabyañ‍janasamudāyai ‘‘sutta’’nti vauhārau, aivaṃ suttasamudāyai ‘‘āgamau’’ti vauhārau| paṭic‍casamuppādādinipuṇatthasabbhāvatau nipuṇassa| āgamissanti aittha, aitaina, aitasmā vā attatthaparatthādayauti āgamau, āgamau ca sau varau cāti āgamavarau, āgamasammataihi vā varauti āgamavarau, tassa| buddhānaṃ anubuddhā buddhānubuddhā, buddhānaṃ sac‍capaṭivaidhaṃ anugamma paṭividdhasac‍cā aggasāvakādayau  ariyā| taihi atthasaṃvaṇṇanāvasaina, guṇasaṃvaṇṇanāvasaina ca saṃvaṇṇitassa| atha vā buddhā ca anubuddhā ca buddhānubuddhāti yaujaitabbaṃ| sammāsambuddhainaiva hi tiṇṇampi piṭakānaṃ atthavaṇṇanāk‍kamau bhāsitau, yā ‘‘pakiṇṇakadaisanā’’ti vuc‍cati, tatau saṅgāyanādivasaina sāvakaihīti ācariyā vadanti|

saddhāvahaguṇassāti buddhādīsu pasādāvahasampattikassa| ayañhi āgamau brahmajālādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.5-7, 26-28) sīladiṭṭhādīnaṃ anavasaisaniddaisādivasaina, mahāpadānādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.3-5) purimabuddhānampi guṇaniddaisādivasaina, pāthikasuttādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 3.3,4) titthiyai nimadditvā appaṭivattiyasīhanāda nadanādivasaina, anuttariyasuttādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 6.8) ca visaisatau buddhaguṇavibhāvanaina ratanattayai sātisayappasādaṃ āvahati| saṃvaṇṇanāsu cāyaṃ ācariyassa pakati, yā taṃtaṃsaṃvaṇṇanāsu āditau tassa tassa saṃvaṇṇaitabbassa dhammassa visaisaguṇakittanaina thaumanā| tathā hi papañ‍casūdanīsāratthappakāsinīmanaurathapūraṇīsu aṭṭhasālinīādīsu ca yathāk‍kamaṃ ‘‘paravādamathanassa ñāṇappabhaidajananassa  dhammakathikapuṅgavānaṃ vicittappaṭibhānajananassa tassa gambhīrañāṇaihi augāḷhassa abhiṇhasau nānānayavicittassa abhidhammassā’’tiādinā thaumanā katā|

atthau kathīyati aitāyāti atthakathā, sā aiva aṭṭhakathā, ttha-kārassa ṭṭha-kāraṃ katvā, yathā ‘‘dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.8)| āditau tiādimhi paṭhamasaṅgītiyaṃ| chaḷabhiñ‍ñatāya paramaina cittavasībhāvaina saman‍nāgatattā, jhānādīsu pañ‍cavidhavasitāsabbhāvatau ca vasinau, thairā mahākassapādayau| taisaṃ sataihi pañ‍cahi| yāti yā aṭṭhakathā| saṅgītāti atthaṃ pakāsaituṃ yuttaṭṭhānai ‘‘ayaṃ aitassa atthau, ayaṃ aitassa atthau’’ti saṅgahaitvā vuttā| anusaṅgītā  ca yasatthairādīhi  pacchāpi dutiyatatiyasaṅgītīsu, iminā attanau saṃvaṇṇanāya āgamanasuddhiṃ dassaiti|

sīhassa lānatau gahaṇatau sīhaḷau, sīhakumārau| taṃvaṃsajātatāya tambapaṇṇidīpai khattiyānaṃ, taisaṃ nivāsatāya tambapaṇṇidīpassa ca sīhaḷabhāvau vaiditabbau| ābhatāti jambudīpatau ānītā| athāti pacchā| aparabhāgai hi asaṅkaratthaṃ sīhaḷabhāsāya aṭṭhakathā ṭhapitāti| tainassa mūlaṭṭhakathā sabbasādhāraṇā na hautīti idaṃ atthappakāsanaṃ aikantaina karaṇīyanti dassaiti| tainaivāha – ‘‘dīpavāsīnamatthāyā’’ti| tattha dīpavāsīnanti jambudīpavāsīnaṃ| dīpavāsīnanti vā sīhaḷadīpavāsīnaṃ atthāya sīhaḷabhāsāya ṭhapitāti yaujanā|

apanaitvānāti kañ‍cukasadisaṃ sīhaḷabhāsaṃ apanaitvā| tatauti aṭṭhakathātau| ahanti attānaṃ niddisati| manauramaṃ bhāsanti māgadhabhāsaṃ| sā hi sabhāvaniruttibhūtā paṇḍitānaṃ manaṃ ramayatīti| tainaivāha – ‘‘tantinayānucchavika’’nti, pāḷigatiyā anulaumikaṃ pāḷibhāsāyānuvidhāyininti atthau| vigatadausanti asabhāvaniruttibhāsantararahitaṃ|

samayaṃ avilaumaintauti siddhantaṃ aviraudhaintau, aitaina atthadausābhāvamāha| aviruddhattā aiva hi thairavādāpi idha pakāsiyissanti| thairavaṃsapadīpānanti thiraihi sīlakkhandhādīhi saman‍nāgatattā thairā, mahākassapādayau| taihi āgatā ācariyaparamparā thairavaṃsau, tappariyāpan‍nā  hutvā āgamādhigamasampan‍nattā pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautaina tassa samuj‍jalanatau thairavaṃsapadīpā, mahāvihāravāsinau thairā, taisaṃ| vividhaihi ākāraihi nicchīyatīti vinicchayau, gaṇṭhiṭṭhānaisu khīlamaddanākāraina pavattā vimaticchaidakathā| suṭṭhu nipuṇau saṇhau vinicchayau aitaisanti sunipuṇavinicchayā|  atha vā vinicchinautīti vinicchayau, yathāvuttavisayaṃ ñāṇaṃ| suṭṭhu nipuṇau chaikau vinicchayau aitaisanti sunipuṇavinicchayā, aitaina mahākassapādithairaparamparābhatau, tatauyaiva ca aviparītau saṇhasukhumau mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ  vinicchayauti tassa pamāṇabhūtataṃ dassaiti|

sujanassa cāti ca-saddau sampiṇḍanatthau, taina na kaivalaṃ jambudīpavāsīnamaiva atthāya, atha khau sādhujanatausanatthañ‍cāti dassaiti, taina ca tambapaṇṇidīpavāsīnampi atthāyāti ayamatthau siddhau hauti, uggahaṇādisukaratāya taisampi bahupakārattā| ciraṭṭhitatthanti ciraṭṭhitiatthaṃ, cirakālaṭṭhitiyāti atthau| idañhi atthappakāsanaṃ aviparītabyañ‍janasunikkhaipassa atthasunayassa ca upāyabhāvatau saddhammassa ciraṭṭhitiyā saṃvattati| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai dvai? sunikkhattañ‍ca padabyañ‍janaṃ, atthau ca sunītau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 2.21)|

yaṃ atthavaṇṇanaṃ kattukāmau, tassā mahattaṃ pariharituṃ ‘‘sīlakathā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tainaivāha – ‘‘na taṃ idha vicārayissāmī’’ti| atha vā yaṃ aṭṭhakathaṃ kattukāmau, tadaikadaisabhāvaina visuddhimaggau ca gahaitabbauti kathikānaṃ upadaisaṃ karauntau tattha vicāritadhammai uddaisavasaina dassaiti ‘‘sīlakathā’’ tiādinā| tattha sīlakathāti cārittavārittādivasaina sīlavitthārakathā| dhutadhammāti piṇḍapātikaṅgādayau (visuddhi॰ 1.22; thairagā॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.845, 849) tairasa kilaisadhunanakadhammā| kammaṭṭhānāni sabbānīti pāḷiyaṃ āgatāni aṭṭhatiṃsa, aṭṭhakathāyaṃ dvaiti niravasaisāni yaugakammassa bhāvanāya pavattiṭṭhānāni| cariyāvidhānasahitauti rāgacaritādīnaṃ sabhāvādividhānaina sahitau| jhānāni cattāri rūpāvacarajjhānāni, samāpattiyau  catassau arūpasamāpattiyau| aṭṭhapi vā paṭiladdhamattāni jhānāni, samāpaj‍janavasībhāvappattiyā samāpattiyau| jhānāni vā rūpārūpāvacarajjhānāni, samāpattiyau phalasamāpattiniraudhasamāpattiyau|

laukiyalaukuttarabhaidā cha abhiñ‍ñāyau sabbā abhiñ‍ñāyau| ñāṇavibhaṅgādīsu āgatanayaina aikavidhādinā pañ‍ñāya saṅkalaitvā sampiṇḍaitvā nicchayau pañ‍ñāsaṅkalananicchayau|

pac‍cayadhammānaṃ haitādīnaṃ pac‍cayuppan‍nadhammānaṃ haitupac‍cayādibhāvau pac‍cayākārau, tassa daisanā pac‍cayākāradaisanā, paṭic‍casamuppādakathāti atthau| sā pana ghanavinibbhaugassa suduk‍karatāya saṇhasukhumā, nikāyantaraladdhisaṅkararahitā, aikattanayādisahitā ca tattha vicāritāti āha – ‘‘suparisuddhanipuṇanayā’’ti| paṭisambhidādīsu āgatanayaṃ avissaj‍jaitvāva vicāritattā avimuttatanti maggā|

iti  pana sabbanti iti-saddau parisamāpanai, pana-saddau vacanālaṅkārai, aitaṃ sabbanti atthau| idhāti imissā aṭṭhakathāyaṃ| na vicārayissāmi, punaruttibhāvatauti adhippāyau|

idāni tassaiva avicāraṇassa aikantakāraṇaṃ niddhāraintau ‘‘majjhai visuddhimaggau’’tiādimāha| tattha ‘‘majjhai ṭhatvā’’ti aitaina majjhaibhāvadīpanaina visaisatau catun‍naṃ āgamānaṃ sādhāraṇaṭṭhakathā visuddhimaggau, na sumaṅgalavilāsinīādayau viya asādhāraṇaṭṭhakathāti dassaiti| ‘‘visaisatau’’ti idaṃ vinayābhidhammānampi visuddhimaggau yathārahaṃ atthavaṇṇanā hauti yaivāti katvā vuttaṃ|

ic‍caivāti iti aiva| tampīti visuddhimaggampi| aitāyāti sumaṅgalavilāsiniyā| aittha ca ‘‘sīhaḷadīpaṃ ābhatā’’tiādinā atthappakāsanassa nimittaṃ dassaiti, ‘‘dīpavāsīnamatthāya, sujanassa ca tuṭṭhatthaṃ, ciraṭṭhitatthañ‍ca dhammassā’’ti aitaina payaujanaṃ, avasiṭṭhaina karaṇappakāraṃ| sīlakathādīnaṃ avicāraṇampi hi idha karaṇappakārau aivāti|

ganthārambhakathāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

nidānakathāvaṇṇanā

vibhāgavantānaṃ  sabhāvavibhāvanaṃ vibhāgadassanavasainaiva hautīti paṭhamaṃ tāva vaggasuttavasaina  vibhāgaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha dīghāgamau nāmā’’tiādimāha| tattha tatthāti ‘‘dīghassa āgamavarassa atthaṃ pakāsayissāmī’’ti yadidaṃ vuttaṃ, tasmiṃ vacanai| yassa atthaṃ pakāsayissāmīti paṭiñ‍ñātaṃ, sau dīghāgamau nāma vaggasuttavasaina aivaṃ vibhāgauti atthau| atha vā tatthāti ‘‘dīghāgamanissitamattha’’nti aitasmiṃ vacanai| yau dīghāgamau vuttau, sau vaggādivasaina aidisauti atthau| attanau saṃvaṇṇanāya paṭhamamahāsaṅgītiyaṃ nikkhittānuk‍kamainaiva pavattabhāvadassanatthaṃ ‘‘tassa vaggaisu…pai॰… vuttaṃ nidānamādī’’ti āha| kasmā pana catūsu āgamaisu dīghāgamau paṭhamaṃ saṅgītau, tattha ca sīlakkhandhavaggau āditau nikkhittau, tasmiñ‍ca brahmajālanti? nāyamanuyaugau katthacipi na pavattati, api ca saddhāvahaguṇatau dīghanikāyau paṭhamaṃ saṅgītau| saddhā hi kusaladhammānaṃ bījaṃ| yathāha – ‘‘saddhā bījaṃ tapau vuṭṭhī’’ti, (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.197; su॰ ni॰ 77) saddhāvahaguṇatā cassa dassitāyaiva| kiñ‍ca katipayasuttasaṅgahatau, appaparimāṇatau ca gahaṇadhāraṇādisukhatau| tathāhaisa catuttiṃsasuttasaṅgahau catusaṭṭhibhāṇavāraparimāṇau ca| sīlakathābāhul‍latau pana sīlakkhandhavaggau paṭhamaṃ nikkhittau| sīlañhi sāsanassa ādi, sīlapatiṭṭhānattā sabbaguṇānaṃ| tainaivāha – ‘‘tasmā tiha, tvaṃ bhikkhu, ādimaiva visaudhaihi kusalaisu dhammaisu| kau cādi kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? sīlañ‍ca suvisuddha’’ntiādi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.395)| aitaina cassa vaggassa anvatthasañ‍ñatā vuttā hauti| diṭṭhivinivaiṭhanakathābhāvatau pana suttantapiṭakassa niravasaisadiṭṭhivibhajanaṃ brahmajālaṃ paṭhamaṃ nikkhittanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| taipiṭakai hi buddhavacanai brahmajālasadisaṃ diṭṭhigatāni niggumbaṃ nij‍jaṭaṃ katvā vibhattasuttaṃ natthīti|

paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikathāvaṇṇanā

yassā  paṭhamamahāsaṅgītiyaṃ nikkhittānuk‍kamaina saṃvaṇṇanaṃ kattukāmau, taṃ, tassā ca tantiāruḷhāya idha vacanai kāraṇaṃ dassaintau ‘‘paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti…pai॰… vaiditabbā’’ti āha| tattha yathāpac‍cayaṃ tattha tattha daisitattā, pañ‍ñattattā ca vippakiṇṇānaṃ dhammavinayānaṃ saṅgahaitvā gāyanaṃ kathanaṃ saṅgīti, aitaina taṃtaṃsikkhāpadānaṃ suttānañ‍ca ādipariyausānaisu, antarantarā ca sambandhavasaina ṭhapitaṃ saṅgītikāravacanaṃ saṅgahitaṃ hauti| mahāvisayattā, pūjanīyattā ca mahatī saṅgīti mahāsaṅgīti, paṭhamā mahāsaṅgīti paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti, tassā pavattikālau paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālau, tasmiṃ paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālai| nidānanti ca daisanaṃ daisakālādivasaina aviditaṃ viditaṃ katvā nidassaitīti nidānaṃ| sattānaṃ dassanānuttariyasaraṇādipaṭilābhahaitubhūtāsu vij‍jamānāsupi añ‍ñāsu bhagavatau kiriyāsu ‘‘buddhau baudhaiyya’’nti (bu॰ vaṃ॰ aṭṭha॰ ratanacaṅkamanakaṇḍavaṇṇanā; cariyā॰ uddhānagāthāvaṇṇanā) paṭiñ‍ñāya anulaumatau vainaiyyānaṃ maggaphalappattīnaṃ haitubhūtā kiriyā nippariyāyaina buddhakic‍canti āha – ‘‘dhammacak‍kappavattanañhi ādiṃ katvā’’ti| tattha saddhindriyādidhammauyaiva pavattanaṭṭhaina cak‍kanti dhammacak‍kaṃ| atha vā cak‍kanti āṇā, dhammatau anapaitattā dhammañ‍ca taṃ cak‍kañ‍cāti dhammacak‍kaṃ, dhammaina ñāyaina cak‍kantipi dhammacak‍kaṃ| yathāha –

‘‘dhammañ‍ca pavattaiti cak‍kañ‍cāti dhammacak‍kaṃ, cak‍kañ‍ca pavattaiti dhammañ‍cāti dhammacak‍kaṃ, dhammaina pavattaitīti dhammacak‍kaṃ, dhammacariyāya pavattaitīti dhammacak‍ka’’ntiādi (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2, 39, 41)|

‘‘katabuddhakic‍cai’’ti aitaina buddhakattabbassa kassacipi asaisitabhāvaṃ dassaiti| nanu ca sāvakaihi vinītāpi vinaiyyā  bhagavatāyaiva vinītā haunti, yatau sāvakabhāsitaṃ suttaṃ ‘‘buddhavacana’’nti vuc‍cati, sāvakavinaiyyā ca na tāva vinītāti? nāyaṃ dausau taisaṃ vinayanupāyassa sāvakaisu ṭhapitattā| tainaivāha –

‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai bhikkhū na sāvakā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ saha  dhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissa’’ntiādi (dī॰ ni॰ 2.168; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.822; udā॰ 51)|

‘‘kusinārāya’’ntiādi bhagavatau parinibbutadaisakālavisaisadassanaṃ ‘‘aparinibbutau bhagavā’’ti gāhassa micchābhāvadassanatthaṃ, laukai jātasaṃvaddhabhāvadassanatthañ‍ca| tathā hi manussabhāvassa supākaṭakaraṇatthaṃ mahābaudhisattā carimabhavai dārapariggahādīnipi karauntīti| upādīyatai kammakilaisaihīti upādi, vipākakkhandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| sau pana upādi kilaisābhisaṅkhāramāranimmathanaina nibbānappattiyaṃ anaussaṭṭhau, idha khandhamac‍cumāranimmathanaina aussaṭṭhau nissaisitauti ayaṃ anupādisaisā, nibbānadhātu| nibbānadhātūti caittha nibbutimattaṃ adhippaitaṃ, itthambhūtalakkhaṇai cāyaṃ karaṇaniddaisau| ‘‘dhātubhājanadivasai’’ti idaṃ na ‘‘san‍nipatitāna’’nti aitassa visaisanaṃ, ussāhajananassa pana visaisanaṃ, ‘‘dhātubhājanadivasai bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhaṃ janaisī’’ti| dhātubhājanadivasatau hi purimapurimataradivasaisu bhikkhū samāgatāti| atha vā dhātubhājanadivasai san‍nipatitānaṃ kāyasāmaggīvasaina sahitānanti atthau| saṅghassa thairau saṅghatthairau, sau pana saṅghau kiṃ parimāṇānanti āha – ‘‘sattan‍naṃ bhikkhusatasahassāna’’nti| nic‍casāpaikkhatāya hi aidisaisu samāsau hautiyaiva, yathā – ‘‘daivadattassa garukula’’nti|

āyasmā mahākassapau puna dul‍labhabhāvaṃ mañ‍ñamānau bhikkhūnaṃ  ussāhaṃ janaisīti sambandhau| ‘‘dhātubhājanadivasai san‍nipatitāna’’nti idaṃ ‘‘bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhaṃ janaisī’’ti aittha ‘‘bhikkhūna’’nti imināpi padaina sambandhanīyaṃ| subhaddaina vuḍḍhapabbajitaina vuttavacanamanussarantauti sambandhau| tattha anussarantau dhammasaṃvaigavasaināti adhippāyau| ‘‘saddhammaṃ antaradhāpaiyyuṃ saṅgāyaiyyaṃ…pai॰… ciraṭṭhitikaṃ tassa kimañ‍ñaṃ āṇaṇyaṃ bhavissatī’’ti aitaisaṃ padānaṃ ‘‘iti cintayantau’’ti aitaina sambandhau| tathā ‘‘yañ‍cāha’’nti aitassa ‘‘anuggahitau pasaṃsitau’’ti aitaina sambandhau| yaṃ pāpabhikkhūti aittha yanti nipātamattaṃ, kāraṇaniddaisau vā, yaina kāraṇaina antaradhāpaiyyuṃ, tadaitaṃ kāraṇaṃ vij‍jatīti atthau, addhaniyanti addhānamaggagāmi, addhānakkhamanti atthau|

yañ‍cāhanti aittha yanti yasmā, yaina kāraṇaināti vuttaṃ hauti, kiriyāparāmasanaṃ vā aitaṃ, taina ‘‘anuggahitau pasaṃsitau’’ti aittha anuggaṇhanaṃ pasaṃsanañ‍ca parāmasati| ‘‘cīvarai sādhāraṇaparibhaugainā’’ti aittha ‘‘attanā samasamaṭṭhapanainā’’ti idha attanā-saddaṃ ānaitvā cīvarai attanā  sādhāraṇaparibhaugaināti yaujaitabbaṃ| yassa yaina hi sambandhau dūraṭṭhampi ca tassa tanti atha vā bhagavatā cīvarai sādhāraṇaparibhaugaina bhagavatā anuggahitauti yaujanīyaṃ, aitassāpi hi karaṇaniddaisassa sahayaugakattutthajautakattasambhavatau| yāvadaiti yāvadaiva, yattakaṃ kālaṃ, yattakai vā samāpattivihārai, abhiñ‍ñāvihārai vā ākaṅkhantau viharāmi caiva vauharāmi ca, tathā kassapaupīti atthau| idañ‍ca navānupubbavihārachaḷabhiñ‍ñabhāvasāmañ‍ñaina thutimattaṃ vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| na hi āyasmā mahākassapau bhagavā viya daivasikaṃ catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassasaṅkhyā samāpattiyau samāpaj‍jati, yamakapāṭihāriyādivasaina vā abhiñ‍ñāyau vaḷañ‍jaitīti| tainaivāha – ‘‘navānupubbavihārachaḷabhiñ‍ñāppabhaidai’’ti| tassa kimañ‍ñaṃ āṇaṇyaṃ bhavissati, añ‍ñatra dhammavinayasaṅgāyanāti adhippāyau| ‘‘nanu maṃ bhagavā’’tiādinā vuttamaivatthaṃ upamāvasaina vibhāvaiti|

tatau paranti tatau bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhajananatau paratau| purai adhammau dippatīti apināma dibbati, yāva adhammau dhammaṃ paṭibāhituṃ samatthau hauti, tatau puraitaramaivāti atthau| āsan‍nai anicchitai hi ayaṃ purai-saddau| dippatīti ca dippissati| puraisaddasan‍niyaugaina hi anāgatatthai ayaṃ vattamānappayaugau, yathā – ‘‘purā vassati daivau’’ti|

‘‘sakalanavaṅgasatthusāsanapariyattidharai…pai॰… aikūnapañ‍casatai pariggahaisī’’ti aitaina sukkhavipassakakhīṇāsavapariyantānaṃ yathāvuttapuggalānaṃ satipi āgamādhigamasabbhāvai saha paṭisambhidāhi pana taivij‍jādiguṇayuttānaṃ āgamādhigamasampattiyā uk‍kaṃsagatattā saṅgītiyā bahupakārataṃ dassaiti| idaṃ vuttaṃ saṅgītikkhandhakai, (pārā॰ 437) apac‍cakkhaṃ nāma natthi paguṇappavattibhāvatau, samantapāsādikāyaṃ pana ‘‘asammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ nāma natthī’’ti (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikathā) vuttaṃ, taṃ ‘‘dvai sahassāni bhikkhutau’’ti vuttampi bhagavatau santikai paṭiggahitamaivāti katvā vuttaṃ| caturāsītisahassānīti dhammakkhandhai sandhāyāha| pavattinauti paguṇāni| ānandatthairassa navappāyāya parisāya vibbhamanaina mahākassapatthairau aivamāha – ‘‘na vāyaṃ kumārakau mattamañ‍ñāsī’’ti| tattha mattanti pamāṇaṃ| chandā āgamanaṃ viyāti padavibhāgau| ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi saikkhau’’ti idaṃ  na saikkhānaṃ agatigamanasabbhāvaina vuttaṃ, asaikkhānamaiva pana uc‍cinitattāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| paṭhamamaggainaiva hi cattāri agatigamanāni pahīyantīti| ‘‘abhabbau chandā…pai॰… agatiṃ gantu’’nti ca dhammasaṅgītiyā tassa yaugyabhāvadassanaina vij‍jamānaguṇakathanaṃ| pariyattauti adhītau|

gāvau  caranti aitthāti gaucarau, gaucarau viya gaucarau, bhikkhācaraṇaṭṭhānaṃ| visabhāgapuggalau subhaddasadisau| sattipañ‍jaranti sattikhaggādihatthaihi purisaihi mal‍larājūnaṃ bhagavatau dhātuārakkhakaraṇaṃ sandhāyāha| taṃ palibaudhaṃ chinditvā taṃ karaṇīyaṃ karautūti saṅgāhakaina chinditabbaṃ chinditvā aikantakaraṇīyaṃ karautūti atthau| mahājananti bahujanaṃ| gandhakuṭiṃ vanditvā paribhaugacaitiyabhāvatauti adhippāyau| yathā tanti yathā añ‍ñaupi yathāvuttasabhāvau, aivanti atthau| saṃvaijaisīti ‘‘nanu bhagavatā paṭikac‍caiva akkhātaṃ – ‘sabbaihaiva piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau’’’tiādinā (dī॰ ni॰ 2.183; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.379; a॰ ni॰ 10.48; cūḷava॰ 437) saṃvaigaṃ janaisi| ussan‍nadhātukanti upacitadausaṃ| bhaisaj‍jamattāti appakaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ| appatthau hi ayaṃ mattā-saddau, ‘‘mattāsukhaparic‍cāgau’’tiādīsu (dha॰ pa॰ 290) viya| dutiyadivasaiti daivatāya saṃvaijitadivasatau, jaitavanavihāraṃ paviṭṭhadivasatau vā dutiyadivasai| āṇāva cak‍kaṃ āṇācak‍kaṃ|

aitadagganti aisau aggau| liṅgavipal‍lāsaina hi ayaṃ niddaisau| yadidanti ca yau ayaṃ, yadidaṃ khandhapañ‍cakanti vā yaujaitabbaṃ| ‘‘paṭhamaṃ  āvusau upāli pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñatta’’nti kasmā vuttaṃ, nanu tassa saṅgītiyā purimakālai paṭhamabhāvau na yuttauti? nau na yuttau, bhagavatā pañ‍ñattānuk‍kamaina pātimaukkhuddaisānuk‍kamaina ca paṭhamabhāvassa siddhattā| yaibhuyyaina hi tīṇi piṭakāni bhagavatau dharamānakālai ṭhitānuk‍kamainaiva saṅgītāni, visaisatau vinayābhidhammapiṭakānīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘vatthumpi pucchī’’tiādi ‘kattha pañ‍ñatta’ntiādinā dassitaina saha tadavasiṭṭhampi saṅgahaitvā dassanavasaina vuttaṃ| paṭhamapārājikaiti paṭhamapārājikapāḷiyaṃ (pārā॰ 24), tainaivāha – ‘‘na hi tathāgatā aikabyañ‍janampi niratthakaṃ vadantī’’ti|

jātakādikai  khuddakanikāyapariyāpan‍nai, yaibhuyyaina ca dhammaniddaisabhūtai tādisai abhidhammapiṭakai saṅgaṇhituṃ yuttaṃ, na pana dīghanikāyādippakārai suttantapiṭakai, nāpi pañ‍ñattiniddaisabhūtai vinayapiṭakaiti dīghabhāṇakā ‘‘jātakādīnaṃ abhidhammapiṭakai saṅgahau’’ti vadanti| cariyāpiṭakabuddhavaṃsānañ‍caittha aggahaṇaṃ, jātakagatikattā| majjhimabhāṇakā pana ‘‘aṭṭhuppattivasaina daisitānaṃ jātakādīnaṃ yathānulaumadaisanābhāvatau tādisai suttantapiṭakai saṅgahau yuttau, na pana sabhāvadhammaniddaisabhūtai yathādhammasāsanai abhidhammapiṭakai’’ti jātakādīnaṃ suttantapiṭakapariyāpan‍nataṃ kathayanti| tattha ca yuttaṃ vicāraitvā gahaitabbaṃ|

aivaṃ  nimittapayaujanakāladaisakārakakaraṇappakāraihi paṭhamaṃ saṅgītiṃ dassaitvā idāni tattha vavatthāpitasiddhaisu dhammavinayaisu nānappakārakausal‍latthaṃ aikavidhādibhaidai dassaituṃ ‘‘aivamaita’’ntiādimāha| tattha vimuttirasanti vimuttiguṇaṃ, vimuttisampattikaṃ vā, aggaphalanipphādanatau, vimuttikic‍caṃ vā, kilaisānaṃ  ac‍cantaṃ vimuttisampādanatau| kaici pana ‘‘vimuttiassāda’’nti vadanti|

kiñ‍cāpi avisaisaina sabbampi buddhavacanaṃ kilaisavinayanaina vinayau, yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānai apāyapatanāditau dhāraṇaina dhammau, idhādhippaitai pana dhammavinayai niddhāraituṃ ‘‘tattha vinayapiṭaka’’ntiādimāha| avasaisaṃ buddhavacanaṃ dhammau, khandhādivasaina sabhāvadhammadaisanābāhul‍latau| atha vā yadipi dhammauyaiva vinayaupi, pariyattiyādibhāvatau, vinayasaddasan‍nidhānai pana bhin‍nādhikaraṇabhāvaina payuttau dhamma-saddau vinayatantividhuraṃ tantiṃ dīpaiti yathā ‘‘puñ‍ñañāṇasambhārā, gaubalibaddha’’nti ca|

‘‘anaikajātisaṃsāra’’nti ayaṃ gāthā bhagavatā attanau sabbañ‍ñutañāṇapadaṭṭhānaṃ arahattappattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhantaina aikūnavīsatimassa pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇassa anantaraṃ bhāsitā| taināha ‘‘idaṃ paṭhamabuddhavacana’’nti| idaṃ kira sabbabuddhaihi avijahitaṃ udānaṃ| ayamassa saṅkhaipatthau – ahaṃ imassa attabhāvagaihassa kārakaṃ taṇhāvaḍḍhakiṃ gavaisantau yaina ñāṇaina taṃ daṭṭhuṃ sak‍kā, tassa baudhiñāṇassatthāya dīpaṅkarapādamūlai katābhinīhārau  aittakaṃ kālaṃ anaikajātisaṃsāraṃ anaikajātisatasahassasaṅkhyaṃ saṃsāravaṭṭaṃ anibbisaṃ taṃ ñāṇaṃ avindantau alabhantauyaiva sandhāvissaṃ saṃsariṃ| yasmā jarāvyādhimaraṇamissatāya jāti nāmaisā punappunaṃ upagantuṃ dukkhā, na ca sā tasmiṃ adiṭṭhai nivattati, tasmā taṃ gavaisantau sandhāvissanti atthau| diṭṭhausīti idāni mayā sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ paṭivijjhantaina diṭṭhau asi| puna gaihanti puna imaṃ attabhāvasaṅkhātaṃ mama gaihaṃ| na kāhasi na karissasi| tava sabbā avasaisākilaisaphāsukā mayā bhaggā| imassa tayā katassa attabhāvagaihassa kūṭaṃ avij‍jāsaṅkhātaṃ kaṇṇikamaṇḍalaṃ visaṅkhataṃ viddhaṃsitaṃ| visaṅkhāraṃ nibbānaṃ ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina gataṃ anupaviṭṭhaṃ idāni mama cittaṃ, ahañ‍ca  taṇhānaṃ khayasaṅkhātaṃ arahattamaggaṃ ajjhagā adhigatau pattausmīti| ayaṃ manasā pavattitadhammānamādi| ‘‘yadā havai pātubhavanti dhammā’’ti (udā॰ 1, 2, 3) ayaṃ pana vācāya pavattitadhammānaṃ ādīti vadanti| antaujappanavasaina kira bhagavā ‘‘anaikajātisaṃsāra’’ntiādimāha (dha॰ pa॰ 153)| ‘‘pāṭipadadivasai’’ti idaṃ ‘‘sabbañ‍ñubhāvappattassā’’ti  na aitaina sambandhitabbaṃ, ‘‘pac‍cavaikkhantassa uppan‍nā’’ti aitaina pana sambandhitabbaṃ| visākhapuṇṇamāyamaiva hi bhagavā pac‍cūsasamayai sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pattauti|

vayadhammāti anic‍calakkhaṇamukhaina dukkhānattalakkhaṇampi saṅkhārānaṃ vibhāvaiti ‘‘yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.15; paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.10) vacanatau| lakkhaṇattayavibhāvananayainaiva ca tadārammaṇaṃ vipassanaṃ dassaintau sabbatitthiyānaṃ avisayabhūtaṃ buddhāvaiṇikaṃ catusac‍cakammaṭṭhānādhiṭṭhānaṃ aviparītaṃ nibbānagāminippaṭipadaṃ pakāsaitīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| idāni tattha sammāpaṭipattiyaṃ niyaujaiti ‘‘appamādaina sampādaithā’’ti| atha vā ‘‘vayadhammā saṅkhārā’’ti aitaina saṅkhaipaina saṃvaijaitvā ‘‘appamādaina sampādaithā’’ti saṅkhaipainaiva niravasaisaṃ sammāpaṭipattiṃ dassaiti| appamādapadañhi sikkhāttayasaṅgahitaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ sāsanaṃ pariyādiyitvā tiṭṭhatīti|

paṭhamasaṅgītiyaṃ asaṅgītaṃ saṅgītikkhandhakakathāvatthuppakaraṇādi| kaici pana ‘‘subhasuttampi (dī॰ ni॰ 1.444) paṭhamasaṅgītiyaṃ asaṅgīta’’nti vadanti, taṃ pana na yuj‍jati| paṭhamasaṅgītitau  puraitaramaiva hi āyasmatā ānandaina jaitavanai viharantaina subhassa māṇavassa bhāsitanti|

daḷhikammasithilīkaraṇappayaujanā yathāk‍kamaṃ pakatisāvaj‍japaṇṇattisāvaj‍jaisu sikkhāpadaisu| taināti vividhanayattādinā| aitanti vividhavisaisanayattāti gāthāvacanaṃ| aitassāti vinayassa|

attatthaparatthādibhaidaiti  yau taṃ suttaṃ sajjhāyati, suṇāti, vācaiti, cintaiti, daisaiti ca, suttaina saṅgahitau sīlādiatthau tassāpi hauti, taina parassa sādhaitabbatau parassāpi hautīti, tadubhayaṃ taṃ suttaṃ sūcaiti dīpaiti| tathā diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaṃ laukiyalaukuttarañ‍cāti aivamādibhaidai atthai ādi-saddaina saṅgaṇhāti| attha-saddau cāyaṃ hitapariyāyavacanaṃ, na bhāsitatthavacanaṃ, yadi siyā, suttaṃ attanaupi bhāsitatthaṃ sūcaiti, parassāpīti ayamatthau vuttau siyā| suttaina ca yau atthau pakāsitau sau tassaiva hautīti, na taina paratthau sūcitau hauti, taina sūcaitabbassa paratthassa nivattaitabbassa abhāvā atthagahaṇañ‍ca na kattabbaṃ| attatthaparatthavinimmuttassa bhāsitatthassa abhāvā ādiggahaṇañ‍ca na kattabbaṃ| tasmā yathāvuttassa hitapariyāyassa atthassa suttai asambhavatau suttadhārassa puggalassa vasaina attatthaparatthā vuttā|

atha  vā suttaṃ anapaikkhitvā yai attatthādayau atthappabhaidā vuttā ‘‘na hañ‍ñadatthatthipasaṃsalābhā’’ti aitassa padassa niddaisai (mahāni॰ 63; cūḷani॰ 85) ‘‘attatthau, paratthau, ubhayatthau, diṭṭhadhammikau atthau, samparāyikau atthau, uttānau atthau, gambhīrau atthau, gūḷhau atthau, paṭicchan‍nau atthau, naiyyau atthau, nītau atthau, anavaj‍jau atthau, nik‍kilaisau atthau, vaudānau atthau, paramatthau’’ti tai suttaṃ sūcaitīti atthau| imasmiṃ atthavikappai attha-saddau bhāsitatthapariyāyaupi hauti| aittha hi purimakā pañ‍ca atthappabhaidā hitapariyāyā, tatau parai cha bhāsitatthabhaidā, pacchimakā pana ubhayasabhāvā| tattha duradhigamatāya vibhāvanai aladdhagādhau gambhīrau|  na vivaṭau gūḷhau| mūludakādayau viya paṃsunā akkharasan‍nivaisādinā tirauhitau paṭicchan‍nau| niddhāraitvā  ñāpaitabbau naiyyau| yathārutavasaina vaiditabbau nītau| anavaj‍janik‍kilaisavaudānā pariyāyavasaina vuttā, kusalavipākakiriyādhammavasaina vā| paramatthau nibbānaṃ, dhammānaṃ aviparītasabhāvau aiva vā| atha vā ‘‘attanā ca appicchau hautī’’ti attatthaṃ, ‘‘appicchākathañ‍ca paraisaṃ kattā hautī’’ti paratthaṃ sūcaiti| aivaṃ ‘‘attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hautī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 4.99, 265) suttāni yaujaitabbāni| vinayābhidhammaihi ca visaisaitvā sutta-saddassa atthau vattabbau| tasmā vainaiyyajjhāsayavasappavattāya daisanāya attahitaparahitatādīni sātisayaṃ pakāsitāni hauti tapparabhāvatau, na āṇādhammasabhāvavasappavattāyāti idamaiva ca ‘‘atthānaṃ sūcanatau sutta’’nti vuttaṃ|

suttai ca āṇādhammasabhāvā ca vainaiyyajjhāsayaṃ anuvattanti, na vinayābhidhammaisu viya vainaiyyajjhāsayau āṇādhammasabhāvai| tasmā vainaiyyānaṃ aikantahitapaṭilābhasaṃvattanikā suttantadaisanā hautīti ‘‘suvuttā caitthā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| pasavatīti phalati| ‘‘suttāṇā’’ti aitassa atthaṃ pakāsaituṃ ‘‘suṭṭhu ca nai tāyatī’’ti vuttaṃ| attatthādividhānaisu ca suttassa pamāṇabhāvau, attatthādīnañ‍ca saṅgāhakattaṃ yaujaitabbaṃ tadatthappakāsanapadhānattā suttassa| vinayābhidhammaihi visaisanañ‍ca yaujaitabbaṃ| aitanti ‘‘atthānaṃ sūcanatau’’tiādikaṃ atthavacanaṃ| aitassāti suttassa|

abhik‍kamantīti aittha abhi-saddau kamanakiriyāya vuddhibhāvaṃ atiraikataṃ dīpaiti, abhiñ‍ñātā abhilakkhitāti aittha ñāṇalakkhaṇakiriyānaṃ supākaṭatāvisaisaṃ, abhik‍kantaināti  aittha kantiyā adhikattaṃ visiṭṭhatanti yuttaṃ kiriyāvisaisakattā upasaggassa| abhirājā abhivinayaiti pana pūjitaparicchin‍naisu rājavinayaisu abhi-saddau pavattatīti kathamaitaṃ yuj‍jaiyyāti? pūjanaparicchaidanakiriyādīpanatau, tāhi ca kiriyāhi rājavinayānaṃ yuttattā| aittha hi atimālādīsu ati-saddau viya, abhi-saddau yathā saha sādhanaina kiriyaṃ vadatīti abhirājaabhivinaya-saddā siddhā, aivaṃ abhidhammasaddai abhi-saddau saha sādhanaina vuḍḍhiyādikiriyaṃ dīpaitīti ayamatthau dassitauti daṭṭhabbau|

bhāvanāpharaṇavuḍḍhīhi  vuḍḍhimantaupi dhammā vuttā| ārammaṇādīhīti ārammaṇasampayuttakammadvārapaṭipadādīhi| avisiṭṭhanti añ‍ñamañ‍ñavisiṭṭhaisu vinayasuttābhidhammaisu avisiṭṭhaṃ samānaṃ| taṃ piṭakasaddanti atthau| yathāvuttaināti ‘‘aivaṃ duvidhatthainā’’tiādinā vuttappakāraina|

kathaitabbānaṃ atthānaṃ daisakāyattaina āṇādividhinā atisaj‍janaṃ pabaudhanaṃ daisanā| sāsitabbapuggalagataina yathāparādhādisāsitabbabhāvaina anusāsanaṃ vinayanaṃ sāsanaṃ| kathaitabbassa saṃvarāsaṃvarādinau atthassa kathanaṃ vacanapaṭibaddhatākaraṇaṃ kathā| kathīyati vā aitthāti kathā| saṃvarāsaṃvarassa kathā saṃvarāsaṃvarakathā| aisa nayau itaraisupi| bhaida-saddau visuṃ visuṃ yaujaitabbau ‘‘daisanābhaidaṃ sāsanabhaidaṃ kathābhaidañ‍ca yathārahaṃ paridīpayai’’ti| bhaidanti ca nānattanti atthau| sikkhā ca pahānāni ca gambhīrabhāvau ca sikkhāppahānagambhīrabhāvaṃ, tañ‍ca paridīpayai| aittha yathāti upārambhanissaraṇadhammakausarakkhaṇahaitupariyāpuṇanaṃ  suppaṭipatti duppaṭipattīti aitaihi pakāraihi| āṇaṃ paṇaituṃ arahatīti āṇārahau sammāsambuddhattā| vauhāraparamatthānampi sabbhāvatau āha āṇābāhul‍latauti| itau paraisupi aisaiva nayau| pacurāparādhā saiyyasakādayau| ajjhāsayau āsayauva atthatau diṭṭhi, ñāṇañ‍ca| vuttañ‍caitaṃ –

‘‘sassatucchaidadiṭṭhi ca, khanti caivānulaumikai|

yathābhūtañ‍ca yaṃ ñāṇaṃ, aitaṃ āsayasaddita’’nti|| (visuddhi॰ ṭī॰ 1.136)|

anusayā kāmarāgabhavarāgadiṭṭhipaṭighavicikicchāmānāvij‍jāvasaina satta anāgatā kilaisā, atītā pac‍cuppan‍nā ca tathaiva vuc‍canti| na hi kālabhaidaina dhammānaṃ sabhāvabhaidau atthīti| cariyāti cha mūlacariyā, antarabhaidaina anaikavidhā, saṃsaggavasaina taisaṭṭhi haunti| tai pana amhaihi asammauhantaradhānasuttaṭīkāyaṃ vibhāgatau dassitā, atthikaihi tatau gahaitabbā| atha vā cariyāti caritaṃ, taṃ sucaritaduc‍caritavasaina duvidhaṃ| adhimutti nāma sattānaṃ pubbaparicayavasaina abhiruci, sā duvidhā hīnapaṇītabhaidaina| ghanavinibbhaugābhāvatau diṭṭhimānataṇhāvasaina  ‘‘ahaṃ mamā’’ti sañ‍ñinau| mahantau saṃvarau asaṃvarau| buddhiatthau hi aya’makārau yathā ‘‘asaikkhā dhammā’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ 11)|

tīsupi  caitaisu aitai dhammatthadaisanā paṭivaidhāti aittha tantiatthau tantidaisanā tantiatthapaṭivaidhau ca tantivisayā hauntīti vinayapiṭakādīnaṃ atthadaisanāpaṭivaidhādhārabhāvau  yuttau, piṭakāni pana tanti yaivāti taisaṃ dhammādhārabhāvau kathaṃ yuj‍jaiyyāti? tantisamudāyassa avayavatantiyā ādhārabhāvatau| avayavassa hi samudāyau ādhārabhāvaina vuc‍cati, yathā – ‘‘rukkhai sākhā’’ti| dhammādīnañ‍ca dukkhaugāhabhāvatau taihi vinayādayau gambhīrāti vinayādīnañ‍ca catubbidhau gambhīrabhāvau vuttau| tasmā dhammādayau aiva dukkhaugāhattā gambhīrā, na vinayādayauti na caudaitabbamaitaṃ samukhaina, visayavisayīmukhaina ca vinayādīnaṃyaiva gambhīrabhāvassa vuttattā| dhammau hi vinayādayau, taisaṃ visayau atthau, dhammatthavisayā ca daisanāpaṭivaidhauti| tattha paṭivaidhassa duk‍karabhāvatau dhammatthānaṃ, daisanāñāṇassa duk‍karabhāvatau daisanāya ca dukkhaugāhabhāvau vaiditabbau, paṭivaidhassa pana uppādaituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyattā, tabbisayañāṇuppattiyā ca duk‍karabhāvatau dukkhaugāhatā vaiditabbā|

‘‘haitumhi ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā’’ti aitaina vacanaina dhammassa haitubhāvau kathaṃ ñātabbauti? ‘‘dhammapaṭisambhidā’’ti aitassa samāsapadassa avayavapadatthaṃ dassaintaina ‘‘haitumhi ñāṇa’’nti vuttattā| ‘‘dhammai paṭisambhidā’’ti aittha hi ‘‘dhammai’’ti aitassa atthaṃ dassaintaina ‘‘haitumhī’’ti vuttaṃ, ‘‘paṭisambhidā’’ti aitassa ca atthaṃ dassaintaina ‘‘ñāṇa’’nti| tasmā haitudhamma-saddā aikatthā, ñāṇapaṭisambhidā-saddā cāti imamatthaṃ vadantaina sādhitau dhammassa haitubhāvau, atthassa haituphalabhāvau ca aivamaiva daṭṭhabbau|

yathādhammanti caittha dhamma-saddau haituṃ haituphalañ‍ca sabbaṃ saṅgaṇhāti| sabhāvavācakau haisa, na pariyattihaitubhāvavācakau, tasmā yathādhammanti yau yau avij‍jāsaṅkhārādidhammau, tasmiṃ tasminti atthau| dhammānurūpaṃ vā yathādhammaṃ| daisanāpi hi paṭivaidhau viya aviparītasavisayavibhāvanatau dhammānurūpaṃ pavattati, yatau ‘aviparītābhilāpau’ti vuc‍cati| dhammābhilāpauti atthabyañ‍janakau aviparītābhilāpau, aitaina ‘‘tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā’’ti (vibha॰ 718) aittha vuttaṃ sabhāvadhammaniruttiṃ dassaiti, saddasabhāvattā daisanāya| tathā hi niruttipaṭisambhidāya  parittārammaṇādibhāvau paṭisambhidāvibhaṅgapāḷiyaṃ (vibha॰ 749) vuttau| aṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘taṃ sabhāvaniruttiṃ saddaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā’’tiādinā (vibha॰ aṭṭha॰ 642) saddārammaṇatā  dassitā| ‘‘imassa atthassa ayaṃ saddau vācakau’’ti vacanavacanīyai vavatthapaitvā taṃtaṃvacanīya vibhāvanavasaina pavattitau hi saddau daisanāti| ‘‘anulaumādivasaina vā kathana’’nti aitaina tassā dhammaniruttiyā abhilāpaṃ kathanaṃ tassa vacanassa pavattanaṃ dassaiti| ‘‘adhippāyau’’ti aitaina ‘‘daisanāti pañ‍ñattī’’ti aitaṃ vacanaṃ dhammaniruttābhilāpaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ, na tabbinimuttaṃ pañ‍ñattiṃ sandhāyāti dassaiti|

nanu ca ‘‘dhammau tantī’’ti imasmiṃ pakkhai dhammassa saddasabhāvattā dhammadaisanānaṃ visaisau na siyāti? na, taisaṃ taisaṃ atthānaṃ baudhakabhāvaina ñātau, uggahaṇādivasaina ca pubbai vavatthāpitau saddappabandhau dhammau, pacchā paraisaṃ avabaudhanatthaṃ pavattitau tadatthappakāsakau  saddau daisanāti| atha vā yathāvuttasaddasamuṭṭhāpakau cittuppādau daisanā, musāvādādayau viya| ‘‘vacanassa pavattana’’nti ca yathāvuttacittuppādavasaina yuj‍jati| sau hi vacanaṃ pavattaiti, tañ‍ca taina pavattīyati daisīyati| ‘‘sau ca laukiyalaukuttarau’’ti aivaṃ vuttaṃ abhisamayaṃ yaina pakāraina abhisamaiti, yaṃ abhisamaiti, yau ca tassa sabhāvau, taihi pākaṭaṃ kātuṃ ‘‘visayatau asammauhatau ca atthānurūpaṃ dhammaisū’’tiādimāha| tattha hi visayatau atthādianurūpaṃ dhammādīsu avabaudhau avij‍jādidhammasaṅkhārādiatthatadubhayapañ‍ñāpanārammaṇau laukiyau abhisamayau, asammauhatau atthādianurūpaṃ dhammādīsu avabaudhau nibbānārammaṇau maggasampayuttau yathāvuttadhammatthapañ‍ñattīsu sammauhaviddhaṃsanau laukuttarau abhisamayauti| abhisamayatau añ‍ñampi paṭivaidhatthaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘taisaṃ taisaṃ vā’’tiādimāha| ‘paṭivaidhanaṃ paṭivaidhau’ti iminā hi vacanatthaina abhisamayau, ‘paṭivijjhīyatīti paṭivaidhau’ti iminā taṃtaṃrūpādidhammānaṃ aviparītasabhāvau ca ‘‘paṭivaidhau’’ti vuc‍catīti|

yathāvuttaihi dhammādīhi piṭakānaṃ gambhīrabhāvaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘idāni yasmā aitaisu piṭakaisū’’tiādimāha| yau caitthāti aitaisu taṃtaṃpiṭakagataisu dhammādīsu yau paṭivaidhau, aitaisu ca piṭakaisu taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ yau aviparītasabhāvauti yaujaitabbaṃ| dukkhaugāhatā ca avij‍jāsaṅkhārādīnaṃ  dhammatthānaṃ duppaṭivijjhatāya, taisaṃ pañ‍ñāpanassa duk‍karabhāvatau taṃdaisanāya, paṭivaidhanasaṅkhātassa paṭivaidhassa uppādanavisayikaraṇānaṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyattā, aviparītasabhāvasaṅkhātassa paṭivaidhassa duviñ‍ñaiyyatāya aiva vaiditabbā|

yanti  yaṃ pariyattiduggahaṇaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| atthanti bhāsitatthaṃ, payaujanatthañ‍ca| na upaparikkhantīti na vicārainti| na nijjhānaṃ khamantīti nijjhānapañ‍ñaṃ nakkhamanti, nijjhāyitvā  pañ‍ñāya disvā raucaitvā gahaitabbā na hauntīti adhippāyau| itīti aivaṃ aitāya pariyattiyā| vādappamaukkhānisaṃsā attanau upari paraihi āraupitavādassa niggahassa pamaukkhappayaujanā hutvā dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti, vādappamaukkhā vā nindāpamaukkhā| yassa catthāyāti yassa ca sīlādipūraṇassa anupādāvimaukkhassa vā atthāya dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti ñāyaina pariyāpuṇantīti adhippāyau| assāti assa dhammassa| nānubhauntīti na vindanti| taisaṃ tai dhammā duggahitattā upārambhamānadabbamakkhapalāsādihaitubhāvaina dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| bhaṇḍāgārai niyuttau bhaṇḍāgārikau, bhaṇḍāgārikau viya bhaṇḍāgārikau, dhammaratanānupālakau| añ‍ñatthaṃ anapaikkhitvā bhaṇḍāgārikassaiva satau pariyatti bhaṇḍāgārikapariyatti|

‘‘tāsaṃyaivā’’ti avadhāraṇaṃ pāpuṇitabbānaṃ chaḷabhiñ‍ñācatuppaṭisambhidādīnaṃ vinayai pabhaidavacanābhāvaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| vairañ‍jakaṇḍai (pārā॰ 12) hi tissau vij‍jāva vibhattā| dutiyai pana ‘‘tāsaṃyaivā’’ti avadhāraṇaṃ catassau paṭisambhidā apaikkhitvā kataṃ, na tissau vij‍jā| tā hi chasu abhiñ‍ñāsu antaugadhāti suttai vibhattā yaivāti|

duggahitaṃ gaṇhāti, ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati anañ‍ña’’ntiādinā (ma॰ ni॰ 1.396)| dhammacintanti  dhammasabhāvavicāraṇaṃ, ‘‘cittuppādamattainaiva dānaṃ hauti, sayamaiva cittaṃ attanau ārammaṇaṃ hauti, sabbaṃ cittaṃ asabhāvadhammārammaṇa’’nti ca aivamādi| taisanti taisaṃ piṭakānaṃ|

aitanti aitaṃ buddhavacanaṃ| atthānulaumatau anulaumikau|  anulaumikataṃyaiva vibhāvaituṃ ‘‘kasmā panā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aikanikāyampīti aikasamūhampi| pauṇikā cikkhal‍likā ca khattiyā, taisaṃ nivāsau pauṇikanikāyau cikkhal‍likanikāyau ca|

navappabhaidanti aittha kathaṃ navappabhaidaṃ? sagāthakañhi suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ, niggāthakañ‍ca suttaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, tadubhayavinimuttañ‍ca suttaṃ udānādivisaisasañ‍ñārahitaṃ natthi, yaṃ suttaṅgaṃ siyā, maṅgalasuttādīnañ‍ca (khu॰ pā॰ 5.2; su॰ ni॰ 225) suttaṅgasaṅgahau na siyā, gāthābhāvatau, dhammapadādīnaṃ viya, gaiyyaṅgasaṅgahau vā siyā, sagāthakattā, sagāthavaggassa viya, tathā ubhatauvibhaṅgādīsu sagāthakappadaisānanti? vuc‍catai –

‘‘suttanti  sāmañ‍ñavidhi, visaisavidhayau parai|

sanimittā niruḷhattā sahatāñ‍ñaina nāñ‍ñatau’’|| (sārattha॰ ṭī॰ 1.paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikathāvaṇṇanā)|

sabbassāpi hi buddhavacanassa suttanti ayaṃ sāmañ‍ñavidhi| tainaivāha āyasmā mahākac‍cānau naittiyaṃ – ‘‘navavidhasuttantapariyaiṭṭhī’’ti (naitti॰ saṅgahavāra)| ‘‘aittakaṃ tassa bhagavatau suttāgataṃ suttapariyāpan‍naṃ (pāci॰ 255, 1242), sakavādai pañ‍casuttasatānī’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā; kathā॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā) aivamādi ca aitassa atthassa sādhakaṃ|

visaisavidhayau  parai sanimittā tadaikadaisaisu gaiyyādayau visaisavidhayau taina taina nimittaina patiṭṭhitā| tathā hi gaiyyassa sagāthakattaṃ tabbhāvanimittaṃ| laukaipi hi sasilaukaṃ sagāthakaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 13) cuṇṇiyaganthaṃ ‘gaiyya’nti vadanti| gāthāvirahai pana sati pucchaṃ katvā vissaj‍janabhāvau vaiyyākaraṇassa tabbhāvanimittaṃ| pucchāvissaj‍janañhi ‘byākaraṇa’nti vuc‍cati, byākaraṇamaiva vaiyyākaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ santai sagāthakādīnampi pucchaṃ katvā vissaj‍janavasaina pavattānaṃ vaiyyākaraṇabhāvau āpaj‍jatīti? nāpaj‍jati, gaiyyādisañ‍ñānaṃ anaukāsabhāvatau, ‘gāthāvirahai satī’ti visaisitattā ca| tathā hi dhammapadādīsu kaivalaṃ gāthābandhaisu, sagāthakattaipi saumanassañāṇamayikagāthāyuttaisu, ‘vuttañhaita’ntiādivacanasambandhaisu, abbhutadhammapaṭisaṃyuttaisu ca suttavisaisaisu yathāk‍kamaṃ gāthāudānaitivuttakaabbhutadhammasañ‍ñā patiṭṭhitā, tathā satipi  gāthābandhabhāvai bhagavatau atītāsu jātīsu cariyānubhāvappakāsakaisu jātakasañ‍ñā, satipi pañhāvissaj‍janabhāvai, sagāthakattai ca kaisuci suttantaisu vaidassa labhāpanatau vaidal‍lasañ‍ñā patiṭṭhitāti aivaṃ taina taina sagāthakattādinā nimittaina taisu taisu suttavisaisaisu gaiyyādisañ‍ñā patiṭṭhitāti visaisavidhayau suttaṅgatau parai gaiyyādayau| yaṃ panaittha gaiyyaṅgādinimittarahitaṃ, taṃ suttaṅgaṃ visaisasañ‍ñāparihāraina sāmañ‍ñasañ‍ñāya pavattanatauti| nanu ca sagāthakaṃ suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ, niggāthakaṃ suttaṃ vaiyyākaraṇanti suttaṅgaṃ na sambhavatīti caudanā tadavatthā vāti? na tadavatthā, saudhitattā| saudhitañhi pubbai gāthāvirahai sati pucchāvissaj‍janabhāvau vaiyyākaraṇassa tabbhāvanimittanti|

yañ‍ca vuttaṃ – ‘‘gāthābhāvatau maṅgalasuttādīnaṃ (khu॰ pā॰ 5.1, 2, 3) suttaṅgasaṅgahau  na siyā’’ti, taṃ na, niruḷhattā|  niruḷhau hi maṅgalasuttādīnaṃ suttabhāvau| na hi tāni dhammapadabuddhavaṃsādayau  viya gāthābhāvaina pañ‍ñātāni, atha khau suttabhāvaina| tainaiva hi aṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘suttanāmaka’’nti nāmaggahaṇaṃ kataṃ| yañ‍ca pana vuttaṃ – ‘‘sagāthakattā gaiyyaṅgasaṅgahau siyā’’ti, tadapi natthi, yasmā sahatāñ‍ñaina| saha gāthāhīti hi sagāthakaṃ| sahabhāvau nāma atthatau añ‍ñaina hauti, na ca maṅgalasuttādīsu kathāvinimuttau kauci suttapadaisau atthi, yau ‘saha gāthāhī’ti vuc‍caiyya, na ca samudāyau nāma kauci atthi, yadapi vuttaṃ – ‘‘ubhatauvibhaṅgādīsu sagāthakappadaisānaṃ gaiyyaṅgasaṅgahau siyā’’ti tadapi na, añ‍ñatau| añ‍ñā aiva hi tā gāthā jātakādipariyāpan‍nattā| atau na tāhi ubhatauvibhaṅgādīnaṃ gaiyyaṅgabhāvauti| aivaṃ suttādīnaṃ aṅgānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñasaṅkarābhāvau vaiditabbau|

‘‘ayaṃ dhammau…pai॰… ayaṃ vinayau, imāni caturāsīti dhammakkhandhasahassānī’’ti buddhavacanaṃ dhammavinayādibhaidaina vavatthapaitvā saṅgāyantaina mahākassapappamukhaina vasigaṇaina anaikacchariyapātubhāvapaṭimaṇḍitāya saṅgītiyā imassa dīghāgamassa paṭhamamajjhimabuddhavacanādibhāvau vavatthāpitauti dassaiti, ‘‘aivamaitaṃ abhaidatau’’tiādinā|

nidānakathāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|



1. brahmajālasuttavaṇṇanā

paribbājakakathāvaṇṇanā

aivaṃ  paṭhamamahāsaṅgītiṃ dassaitvā yadatthaṃ sā idha dassitā, idāni taṃ nigamanavasaina dassaituṃ ‘‘imissā’’tiādimāha|

1. aittāvatā ca brahmajālassa sādhāraṇatau bāhiranidānaṃ dassaitvā idāni abbhantaranidānaṃ saṃvaṇṇaituṃ ‘‘tattha aiva’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| atha vā chahi ākāraihi saṃvaṇṇanā kātabbā sambandhatau padatau padavibhāgatau padatthatau anuyaugatau parihāratau cāti| tattha sambandhau nāma daisanāsambandhau| yaṃ laukiyā ‘‘ummugghātau’’ti vadanti| sau pana pāḷiyā nidānapāḷivasaina, nidānapāḷiyā pana saṅgītivasaina vaiditabbauti paṭhamamahāsaṅgītiṃ dassaintaina nidānapāḷiyā sambandhassa dassitattā padādivasaina saṃvaṇṇanaṃ karauntau ‘‘aivanti nipātapada’’ntiādimāha| ‘‘maitiādīnī’’ti aittha antarā-sadda-ca-saddānaṃ nipātapadabhāvau, vattabbau, na vā vattabbau taisaṃ nayaggahaṇaina gahitattā, tadavasiṭṭhānaṃ āpaṭi-saddānaṃ ādi-saddaina saṅgaṇhanatau| ‘‘padavibhāgau’’ti padānaṃ visaisau, na pana padaviggahau| atha vā padāni ca padavibhāgau ca padavibhāgau, padaviggahau ca padavibhāgau ca padavibhāgauti vā aikasaisavasaina padapadaviggahāpi padavibhāga saddaina vuttāti vaiditabbaṃ| tattha padaviggahau ‘‘bhikkhūnaṃ saṅghau’’tiādibhaidaisu padaisu daṭṭhabbau|

atthatauti padatthatau| taṃ pana padatthaṃ atthuddhārak‍kamaina  paṭhamaṃ aivaṃ-saddassa dassaintau ‘‘aivaṃsaddau tāvā’’tiādimāha| avadhāraṇādīti aittha ādi-saddaina idamatthapucchāparimāṇādiatthānaṃ saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| tathā hi ‘‘aivaṃgatāni, aivaṃvidhau, aivamākārau’’tiādīsu idaṃ-saddassa atthai aivaṃ-saddau| gata-saddau hi pakārapariyāyau, tathā vidhākāra-saddā  ca| tathā hi vidhayuttagata-saddai laukiyā pakāratthai vadanti| ‘‘aivaṃ nu khau, na nu khau, kiṃ nu khau, kathaṃ nu khau’’ti, ‘‘aivaṃ su tai sunhātā suvilittā kappitakaisamassu, āmuttamālābharaṇā audātavatthavasanā pañ‍cahi  kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricārainti, saiyyathāpi tvaṃ aitarahi sācariyakauti? nau hidaṃ bhau gautamā’’ti ca ādīsu pucchāyaṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ lahuparivattaṃ, aivaṃ āyupariyantau’’ti ca ādīsu parimāṇai| nanu ca ‘‘aivaṃ nu khau, aivaṃ su tai, aivaṃ āyupariyantau’’ti aittha aivaṃ-saddaina pucchanākāraparimāṇākārānaṃ vuttattā ākāratthau aiva aivaṃ-saddau ti? na, visaisasabbhāvatau| ākāramattavācakau haittha ākāratthauti adhippaitau, yathā ‘‘aivaṃ byākhautiādīsu pana na ākāravisaisavācakau aivañ‍ca katvā ‘‘aivaṃ jātaina mac‍cainā’’tiādīni upamādīsu udāharaṇāni upapan‍nāni haunti| tathā hi ‘‘yathāpi…pai॰… bahu’’nti? aittha  puppharāsiṭṭhāniyatau manussupapattisappurisūpanissayasaddhammasavanayaunisaumanasikārabhaugasampattiādidānādipuñ‍ñakiriyāhaitusamudāyatau saubhāsugandhatādiguṇayaugatau mālāguṇasadisiyau pahūtā puñ‍ñakiriyā maritabbasabhāvatāya mac‍caina sattaina kattabbāti jauditattā puppharāsimālāguṇāva upamā, taisaṃ upamākārau yathā-saddaina aniyamatau vuttauti aivaṃ-saddau upamākāranigamanatthauti vattuṃ yuttaṃ| sau pana upamākārau niyamiyamānau atthatau upamāva hautīti āha ‘‘upamāyaṃ āgatau’’ti|

tathā aivaṃ iminā ākāraina ‘‘abhik‍kamitabba’’ntiādinā upadisiyamānāya samaṇasāruppāya ākappasampattiyā yau tattha upadisanākārau, sau atthatau upadaisauyaivāti vuttaṃ ‘‘aivaṃ tai…pai॰… upadaisai’’ti| tathā aivamaitaṃ bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ sugatāti aittha ca bhagavatā yathāvuttamatthaṃ aviparītatau jānantaihi kataṃ tattha saṃvij‍jamānaguṇānaṃ pakāraihi haṃsanaṃ udaggatākaraṇaṃ sampahaṃsanaṃ, yau tattha sampahaṃsanākārauti yaujaitabbaṃ| aivamaivaṃ panāyanti aittha garahaṇākārauti yaujaitabbaṃ| sau ca garahaṇākārau ‘‘vasalī’’tiādi khuṃsanasaddasan‍nidhānatau idha aivaṃ-saddaina pakāsitauti viñ‍ñāyati| yathā  caittha, aivaṃ upamākārādayaupi upamādivasaina vuttānaṃ puppharāsiādisaddānaṃ san‍nidhānatauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivañ‍ca vadaihīti ‘‘yathāhaṃ vadāmi, aivaṃ samaṇaṃ ānandaṃ vadaihī’’ti vadanākārau idāni vattabbau aivaṃ-saddaina nidassīyatīti nidassanatthau vuttau| aivaṃ nauti aitthāpi taisaṃ yathāvuttadhammānaṃ ahitadukkhāvahabhāvai san‍niṭṭhānajananatthaṃ anumatiggahaṇavasaina ‘‘saṃvattanti, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti pucchāya katāya ‘‘aivaṃ nau aittha hautī’’ti vuttattā tadākārasan‍niṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ-saddaina vibhāvitanti viñ‍ñāyati, sau pana taisaṃ dhammānaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya  saṃvattanākārau niyamiyamānau avadhāraṇatthau  hautīti āha ‘‘aivaṃ nau aittha hautīti ādīsu avadhāraṇai’’ti| aivaṃ bhantaiti pana dhammassa sādhukaṃ savanamanasikārai san‍niyaujitaihi bhikkhūhi attanau tattha ṭhitabhāvassa paṭijānanavasaina vuttattā aittha aivaṃ-saddau vacanasampaṭicchanatthau vuttau, taina aivaṃ bhantai, sādhu bhantai, suṭṭhu bhantaiti vuttaṃ hauti|

nānānayanipuṇanti aikattanānattaabyāpāraaivaṃdhammatāsaṅkhātā, nandiyāvaṭṭa tipukkhalasīhavik‍kīḷitaaṅkusadisālaucanasaṅkhātā vā ādhārādibhaidavasaina nānāvidhā nayā nānānayā, nayā vā pāḷigatiyau, tā ca pañ‍ñattianupañ‍ñattiādivasaina saṃkilaibhāgiyādilaukiyāditadubhayavaumissatādivasaina kusalādivasaina khandhādivasaina saṅgahādivasaina samayavimuttādivasaina ṭhapanādivasaina kusalamūlādivasaina tikapaṭṭhānādivasaina ca nānappakārāti nānānayā, taihi nipuṇaṃ saṇhasukhumanti nānānayanipuṇaṃ| āsayauva ajjhāsayau, tai ca sassatādibhaidaina, tattha ca apparajakkhatādivasaina anaikā, attajjhāsayādayau aiva vā samuṭṭhānaṃ uppattihaitu aitassāti anaikajjhāsayasamuṭṭhānaṃ| atthabyañ‍janasampan‍nanti atthabyañ‍janaparipuṇṇaṃ upanaitabbābhāvatau, saṅkāsanapakāsanavivaraṇavibhajanauttānīkaraṇapañ‍ñattivasaina chahi atthapadaihi, akkharapadabyañ‍janākāraniruttiniddaisavasaina chahi byañ‍janapadaihi ca saman‍nāgatanti vā atthau daṭṭhabbau|

vividhapāṭihāriyanti aittha pāṭihāriyapadassa vacanatthaṃ ‘‘paṭipakkhaharaṇatau rāgādikilaisāpanayanatau pāṭihāriya’’nti vadanti| bhagavatau pana paṭipakkhā rāgādayau na santi, yai haritabbā| puthuj‍janānampi vigatūpak‍kilaisai  aṭṭhaguṇasaman‍nāgatai cittai hatapaṭipakkhai iddhividhaṃ pavattati, tasmā tattha pavattavauhāraina ca na sak‍kā idha ‘‘pāṭihāriya’’nti vattuṃ| sacai pana mahākāruṇikassa bhagavatau  vainaiyyagatā ca kilaisā paṭipakkhā, taisaṃ haraṇatau ‘‘pāṭihāriya’’nti vuttaṃ, aivaṃ sati yuttamaitaṃ| atha vā bhagavatau ca sāsanassa ca paṭipakkhā titthiyā, taisaṃ haraṇatau pāṭihāriyaṃ| tai hi diṭṭhiharaṇavasaina, diṭṭhippakāsanai asamatthabhāvaina ca iddhiādaisanānusāsanīhi haritā apanītā hauntīti| ‘‘paṭī’’ti vā ayaṃ saddau ‘‘pacchā’’ti aitassa atthaṃ baudhaiti ‘‘tasmiṃ paṭipaviṭṭhamhi, añ‍ñau āgañchi brāhmaṇau’’tiādīsu viya, tasmā samāhitai cittai, vigatūpak‍kilaisai ca katakic‍caina pacchā haritabbaṃ pavattaitabbanti paṭihāriyaṃ, attanau vā upak‍kilaisaisu catutthajjhānamaggaihi haritaisu pacchā haraṇaṃ paṭihāriyaṃ| iddhiādaisanānusāsaniyau ca vigatūpak‍kilaisaina, katakic‍caina ca sattahitatthaṃ puna pavattaitabbā, haritaisu ca attanau upak‍kilaisaisu parasattānaṃ upak‍kilaisaharaṇāni  hauntīti paṭihāriyāni bhavanti| paṭihāriyamaiva pāṭihāriyaṃ| paṭihāriyai vā iddhiādaisanānusāsanīsamudāyai bhavaṃ aikaikaṃ ‘‘pāṭihāriya’’nti vuc‍cati| paṭihāriyaṃ vā catutthajjhānaṃ maggau ca paṭipakkhaharaṇatau, tattha jātaṃ, tasmiṃ vā nimittabhūtai, tatau vā āgatanti pāṭihāriyaṃ| tassa pana iddhiādibhaidaina visayabhaidaina ca bahuvidhassa bhagavatau daisanāya labbhamānattā āha ‘‘vividhapāṭihāriya’’nti|

na añ‍ñathāti bhagavatau sammukhā sutākāratau na añ‍ñathāti atthau, na pana bhagavatau daisitākāratau| acintaiyyānubhāvā hi bhagavatau daisanā| aivañ‍ca katvā ‘‘sabbappakāraina kau samatthau viñ‍ñātu’’nti idaṃ vacanaṃ samatthitaṃ hauti| dhāraṇabaladassanañ‍ca na virujjhati sutākārāvirajjhanassa adhippaitattā| na haittha atthantaratāparihārau dvin‍nampi atthānaṃ aikavisayattā, itarathā thairau bhagavatau daisanāya sabbathā paṭiggahaṇai samatthau asamatthau cāti āpaj‍jaiyyāti|

‘‘yau parau na hauti, sau attā’’ti aivaṃ  vuttāya niyakajjhattasaṅkhātāya sasantatiyaṃ vattanatau tividhaupi mai-saddau kiñ‍cāpi aikasmiṃyaiva  atthai dissati, karaṇasampadānasāminiddaisavasaina pana vij‍jamānabhaidaṃ sandhāyāha ‘‘mai-saddau tīsu atthaisu dissatī’’ti|

kiñ‍cāpi upasaggau kiriyaṃ visaisaiti, jautakabhāvatau pana satipi tasmiṃ suta-saddau aiva taṃ tamatthaṃ anuvadatīti anupasaggassa suta-saddassa atthuddhārai saupasaggassa gahaṇaṃ na virujjhatīti dassaintau ‘‘saupasaggau ca anupasaggau cā’’ti āha| assāti suta-saddassa| kammabhāvasādhanāni idha suta-saddai sambhavantīti vuttaṃ ‘‘upadhāritanti vā upadhāraṇanti vā atthau’’ti| mayāti atthai satīti yadā maisaddassa kattuvasaina karaṇaniddaisau, tadāti atthau| mamāti atthai satīti yadā sambandhavasaina sāminiddaisau, tadā|

sutasaddasan‍nidhānai payuttaina aivaṃsaddaina savanakiriyājautakaina bhavitabbanti vuttaṃ ‘‘aivanti sautaviñ‍ñāṇādiviñ‍ñāṇakic‍canidassana’’nti| ādi-saddaina sampaṭicchanādīnaṃ pañ‍cadvārikaviñ‍ñāṇānaṃ tadabhinihaṭānañ‍ca manaudvārikaviñ‍ñāṇānaṃ gahaṇaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| sabbaisampi vākyānaṃ aivakāratthasahitattā ‘‘suta’’nti aitassa sutaṃ aivāti ayamatthau labbhatīti āha ‘‘assavanabhāvapaṭikkhaipatau’’ti, aitaina avadhāraṇaina nirākataṃ dassaiti| yathā ca sutaṃ sutaṃ aivāti  niyamaitabbaṃ, taṃ sammā sutaṃ hautīti āha ‘‘anūnādhikāviparītaggahaṇanidassana’’nti| atha vā ‘‘saddantaratthāpauhanavasaina saddau atthaṃ vadatī’’ti sutanti asutaṃ na hautīti ayamaitassa atthauti vuttaṃ ‘‘assavanabhāvapaṭikkhaipatau’’ti, iminā diṭṭhādivinivattanaṃ karauti| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti| na idaṃ mayā diṭṭhaṃ, na sayambhuñāṇaina sacchikataṃ, atha khau sutaṃ, tañ‍ca khau sammadaivāti| tainaivāha ‘‘anūnādhikāviparītaggahaṇanidassana’’nti| avadhāraṇatthai vā aivaṃ-saddai ayaṃ atthayaujanā karīyatīti tadapaikkhassa suta-saddassa ayamatthau vuttau ‘‘assavanabhāvapaṭikkhaipatau’’ti| tainaiva āha  ‘‘anūnādhikāviparītaggahaṇanidassana’’nti| savanasaddau caittha kammatthau vaiditabbau suyyatīti|

aivaṃ savanahaitusuṇantapuggalasavanavisaisavasaina padattayassa aikaina pakāraina atthayaujanaṃ dassaitvā idāni pakārantaraihipi taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tathā aiva’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha tassāti yā sā bhagavatau sammukhā dhammassavanākāraina pavattā manaudvāraviñ‍ñāṇavīthi, tassā| sā hi nānappakāraina  ārammaṇai pavattituṃ samatthā| tathā ca vuttaṃ ‘‘sautadvārānusārainā’’ti| nānappakāraināti vakkhamānānaṃ anaikavihitānaṃ byañ‍janatthaggahaṇānānākāraina, aitaina imissā yaujanāya ākāratthau aivaṃ-saddau gahitauti dīpaiti| pavattibhāvappakāsananti pavattiyā atthibhāvappakāsanaṃ| ‘‘sutanti dhammappakāsana’’nti yasmiṃ ārammaṇai vuttappakārā viñ‍ñāṇavīthi nānappakāraina pavattā, tassa dhammattā vuttaṃ, na sutasaddassa dhammatthattā| vuttassaivatthassa pākaṭīkaraṇaṃ ‘‘ayañhaitthā’’tiādi| tattha viñ‍ñāṇavīthiyāti karaṇatthai karaṇavacanaṃ| mayāti katthuatthai|

‘‘aivanti niddisitabbappakāsana’’nti nidassanatthaṃ aivaṃ-saddaṃ gahaitvā vuttaṃ nidassaitabbassa niddisitabbattābhāvābhāvatau, taina aivaṃ-saddaina sakalampi suttaṃ pac‍cāmaṭṭhanti dassaiti| suta-saddassa kiriyāsaddattā, savanakiriyāya ca sādhāraṇaviñ‍ñāṇappabandhapaṭibaddhattā tattha ca puggalavauhārauti vuttaṃ ‘‘sutanti puggalakic‍cappakāsana’’nti| na hi puggalavauhārarahitai dhammappabandhai savanakiriyā labbhatīti|

‘‘yassa cittasantānassā’’tiādipi ākāratthamaiva aivaṃ-saddaṃ gahaitvā purimayaujanāya añ‍ñathā atthayaujanaṃ dassaituṃ vuttaṃ| tattha ākārapañ‍ñattīti upādāpañ‍ñatti aiva, dhammānaṃ pavattiākārupādānavasaina tathā vuttā| ‘‘sutanti visayaniddaisau’’ti sautabbabhūtau dhammau savanakiriyākattupuggalassa savanakiriyāvasaina pavattiṭṭhānanti katvā vuttaṃ| cittasantānavinimuttassa  paramatthatau  kassaci kattu abhāvaipi saddavauhāraina buddhiparikappitabhaidavacanicchāya cittasantānatau añ‍ñaṃ viya taṃsamaṅgiṃ katvā vuttaṃ ‘‘cittasantānaina taṃsamaṅginau’’ti| savanakiriyāvisayaupi sautabbadhammau savanakiriyāvasaina pavattacittasantānassa idha paramatthatau kattubhāvatau, savanavasaina cittappavattiyā aiva vā savanakiriyābhāvatau taṃkiriyākattu ca visayau hautīti katvā vuttaṃ ‘‘taṃsamaṅginau kattu visayai’’ti| sutākārassa ca thairassa sammānicchitabhāvatau āha ‘‘gahaṇasan‍niṭṭhāna’’nti, aitaina vā avadhāraṇatthaṃ aivaṃ-saddaṃ gahaitvā ayaṃ atthayaujanā katāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

pubbai  sutānaṃ nānāvihitānaṃ suttasaṅkhātānaṃ atthabyañ‍janānaṃ upadhāritarūpassa ākārassa nidassanassa avadhāraṇassa vā pakāsanasabhāvau aivaṃ-saddauti tadākārādiupadhāraṇassa puggalapañ‍ñattiyā upādānabhūtadhammappabandhabyāpāratāya vuttaṃ ‘‘aivanti puggalakic‍caniddaisau’’ti| savanakiriyā pana puggalavādinaupi viñ‍ñāṇanirapaikkhā natthīti visaisatau viñ‍ñāṇabyāpārauti āha ‘‘sutanti viñ‍ñāṇakic‍caniddaisau’’ti| maiti saddappavattiyā aikantainaiva sattavisayattā, viñ‍ñāṇakic‍cassa ca tatthaiva samaudahitabbatau ‘‘maiti ubhayakic‍cayuttapuggalaniddaisau’’ti vuttaṃ| avij‍jamānapañ‍ñattivij‍jamānapañ‍ñattisabhāvā yathāk‍kamaṃ aivaṃ-sadda suta-saddānaṃ atthāti tai tathārūpapañ‍ñattiupādānabyāpārabhāvaina dassaintau āha ‘‘aivanti puggalakic‍caniddaisau| sutanti viñ‍ñāṇakic‍caniddaisau’’ti| aittha ca karaṇakiriyākattukammavisaisappakāsanavasaina puggalabyāpāvisayapuggalabyāpāranidassanavasaina gahaṇākāragāhakatabbisayavisaisaniddaisavasaina kattukaraṇa byāpārakattuniddaisavasaina ca dutiyādayau catassau atthayaujanā dassitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

sabbassāpi  saddādhigamanīyassa atthassa pañ‍ñattimukhainaiva paṭipaj‍jitabbattā, sabbapañ‍ñattīnañ‍ca vij‍jamānādivasaina chasu pañ‍ñattibhaidaisu antaugadhattā taisu ‘‘aiva’’ntiādīnaṃ pañ‍ñattīnaṃ sarūpaṃ niddhāraintau āha ‘‘aivanti ca maiti cā’’tiādi| tattha aivanti ca maiti ca vuc‍camānassa atthassa ākārādinau, dhammānañ‍ca asal‍lakkhaṇabhāvatau avij‍jamānapañ‍ñattibhāvauti āha ‘‘sac‍cikaṭṭhaparamatthavasaina avij‍jamānapañ‍ñattī’’ti| tattha sac‍cikaṭṭhaparamatthavasaināti bhūtatthauttamatthavasaina| idaṃ vuttaṃ hautiyau māyāmarīciādayau viya abhūtatthau, anussavādīhi gahaitabbau viya anuttamatthau ca na hauti, sau rūpasaddādisabhāvau ruppanānubhavanādisabhāvau vā atthau ‘‘sac‍cikaṭṭhau, paramattha cā’’ti vuc‍cati, na tathā aivaṃ maiti padānamatthauti, aitamaivatthaṃ pākaṭataraṃ kātuṃ ‘‘kiñhaittha ta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| sutanti pana saddāyatanaṃ sandhāyāha ‘‘vij‍jamānapañ‍ñattī’’ti| tainaiva hi ‘‘yañhi tamaittha sautaina upaladdha’’nti vuttaṃ, ‘‘sautadvārānusāraina upaladdha’’nti pana vuttai atthabyañ‍janādisabbaṃ labbhati| taṃ taṃ upādāya vattabbatauti sautapathaṃ āgatai dhammai upādāya taisaṃ upadhāritākārādinau pac‍cāmasanavasaina ‘‘aiva’’nti, sasantatipariyāpan‍nai khandhai upādāya ‘‘mai’’ti  vattabbattāti atthau| diṭṭhādisabhāvarahitai saddāyatanai pavattamānaupi sutavauhārau ‘‘dutiyaṃ tatiya’’ntiādikau viya paṭhamādīni diṭṭhamutaviñ‍ñātai apaikkhitvā pavattauti āha ‘‘diṭṭhādīni upanidhāya vattabbatau’’ti| asutaṃ na hautīti hi ‘‘suta’’nti pakāsitau yamatthauti|
